





|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

aṅguttaranikāyau

navakanipātapāḷi


1. paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ


1. sambaudhivaggau

1. sambaudhisuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘sambaudhipakkhikānaṃ sambaudhapakkhikānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), āvusau, dhammānaṃ kā upanisā bhāvanāyā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ  paribbājakānaṃ kinti byākaraiyyāthā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti|

‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sacai, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘sambaudhipakkhikānaṃ, āvusau, dhammānaṃ kā upanisā bhāvanāyā’ti, aivaṃ puṭṭhā  tumhai, bhikkhavai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha  –

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| sambaudhipakkhikānaṃ, āvusau, dhammānaṃ ayaṃ paṭhamā upanisā bhāvanāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| sambaudhipakkhikānaṃ, āvusau, dhammānaṃ ayaṃ dutiyā upanisā bhāvanāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu yāyaṃ kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā santuṭṭhikathā pavivaikakathā asaṃsaggakathā vīriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā pañ‍ñākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, aivarūpiyā kathāya nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| sambaudhipakkhikānaṃ, āvusau, dhammānaṃ ayaṃ tatiyā upanisā bhāvanāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| sambaudhipakkhikānaṃ, āvusau, dhammānaṃ ayaṃ catutthī upanisā bhāvanāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya  nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| sambaudhipakkhikānaṃ, āvusau, dhammānaṃ ayaṃ pañ‍camī upanisā bhāvanāya’’|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa – sīlavā bhavissati, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharissati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhissati sikkhāpadaisu|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa – yāyaṃ kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā santuṭṭhikathā pavivaikakathā  asaṃsaggakathā vīriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā pañ‍ñākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, aivarūpiyā kathāya nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhī|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa – āraddhavīriyau viharissati  akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa – pañ‍ñavā bhavissati udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā|

‘‘taina ca pana, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunā imaisu pañ‍casu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya cattārau dhammā uttari uttariṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) bhāvaitabbā – asubhā bhāvaitabbā rāgassa pahānāya, maittā bhāvaitabbā byāpādassa pahānāya, ānāpānassati ānāpānasati (sī॰ pī॰) bhāvaitabbā vitak‍kupacchaidāya, anic‍casañ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā asmimānasamugghātāya| anic‍casañ‍ñinau, bhikkhavai, anattasañ‍ñā saṇṭhāti| anattasañ‍ñī asmimānasamugghātaṃ pāpuṇāti diṭṭhaiva dhammai nibbāna’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. nissayasuttaṃ

2. atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘nissayasampan‍nau nissayasampan‍nau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, bhikkhu nissayasampan‍nau hautī’’ti? ‘‘saddhaṃ cai, bhikkhu, bhikkhu nissāya akusalaṃ pajahati kusalaṃ bhāvaiti, pahīnamaivassa taṃ akusalaṃ hauti| hiriṃ cai, bhikkhu, bhikkhu nissāya…pai॰… auttappaṃ cai, bhikkhu, bhikkhu nissāya…pai॰… vīriyaṃ cai, bhikkhu, bhikkhu nissāya…pai॰… pañ‍ñaṃ cai, bhikkhu, bhikkhu nissāya akusalaṃ pajahati kusalaṃ bhāvaiti, pahīnamaivassa taṃ akusalaṃ hauti| taṃ hissa bhikkhunau akusalaṃ pahīnaṃ hauti suppahīnaṃ, yaṃsa ariyāya pañ‍ñāya disvā pahīnaṃ’’|

‘‘taina  ca pana, bhikkhu, bhikkhunā imaisu pañ‍casu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya cattārau upanissāya vihātabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhu, bhikkhu saṅkhāyaikaṃ paṭisaivati, saṅkhāyaikaṃ adhivāsaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ parivaj‍jaiti, saṅkhāyaikaṃ vinaudaiti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, bhikkhu nissayasampan‍nau hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. maighiyasuttaṃ

3. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā cālikāyaṃ viharati cālikāpabbatai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā maighiyau bhagavatau upaṭṭhākau hauti| atha khau āyasmā maighiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitau khau āyasmā maighiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, jantugāmaṃ jatugāmaṃ (sī॰ aṭṭha॰, syā॰ aṭṭha॰), jattugāmaṃ (ka॰ aṭṭhakathāyampi pāṭhantaraṃ) piṇḍāya  pavisitu’’nti| ‘‘yassa dāni tvaṃ, maighiya, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā maighiyau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya jantugāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| jantugāmai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina kimikāḷāya nadiyā tīraṃ tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau āyasmā maighiyau kimikāḷāya nadiyā  tīrai jaṅghāvihāraṃ jaṅghavihāraṃ (syā॰ ka॰) anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau ambavanaṃ  pāsādikaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ| disvānassa aitadahausi – ‘‘pāsādikaṃ vatidaṃ ambavanaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ, alaṃ vatidaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa padhānāya| sacai maṃ bhagavā anujānaiyya, āgacchaiyyāhaṃ imaṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā maighiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā maighiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya jantugāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| jantugāmai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina kimikāḷāya nadiyā tīraṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃ| addasaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, kimikāḷāya nadiyā tīrai jaṅghāvihāraṃ anucaṅkamamānau anuvicaramānau ambavanaṃ pāsādikaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ| disvāna mai aitadahausi – ‘pāsādikaṃ vatidaṃ ambavanaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ| alaṃ vatidaṃ kulaputtassa padhānatthikassa padhānāya| sacai  maṃ bhagavā anujānaiyya, āgacchaiyyāhaṃ imaṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā’ti| sacai maṃ bhagavā anujānaiyya, gacchaiyyāhaṃ taṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā’’ti| ‘‘āgamaihi tāva, maighiya! aikakamhi aikakamhā (sī॰ pī॰) tāva vata (ka॰) yāva añ‍ñaupi kauci bhikkhu āgacchatī’’ti dissatūti (sabbattha, ṭīkāyampi pāṭhantaraṃ), āgacchatūti, dissatīti (ṭīkāyaṃ pāṭhantarāni)|

dutiyampi khau āyasmā maighiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavatau, bhantai, natthi kiñ‍ci uttari karaṇīyaṃ, natthi katassa paṭicayau| mayhaṃ khau pana, bhantai, atthi uttari karaṇīyaṃ, atthi katassa paṭicayau| sacai maṃ bhagavā anujānaiyya, gacchaiyyāhaṃ taṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā’’ti| ‘‘āgamaihi tāva, maighiya, aikakamhi tāva yāva añ‍ñaupi kauci bhikkhu āgacchatī’’ti|

tatiyampi  khau āyasmā maighiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘bhagavatau, bhantai, natthi kiñ‍ci uttari karaṇīyaṃ, natthi katassa paṭicayau| mayhaṃ khau pana, bhantai, atthi uttari karaṇīyaṃ, atthi katassa paṭicayau| sacai maṃ bhagavā anujānaiyya, gacchaiyyāhaṃ taṃ ambavanaṃ padhānāyā’’ti| ‘‘padhānanti khau, maighiya, vadamānaṃ kinti vadaiyyāma! yassa dāni tvaṃ, maighiya, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā maighiyau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaina taṃ ambavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taṃ ambavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi| atha khau āyasmatau maighiyassa tasmiṃ ambavanai viharantassa yaibhuyyaina tayau pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā samudācaranti, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmavitak‍kau, byāpādavitak‍kau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| atha khau āyasmatau maighiyassa aitadahausi – ‘‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau! saddhāya ca vatamhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā; atha ca panimaihi tīhi pāpakaihi akusalaihi vitak‍kaihi anvāsattā – kāmavitak‍kaina, byāpādavitak‍kaina, vihiṃsāvitak‍kainā’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā maighiyau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā maighiyau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idha  mayhaṃ, bhantai, tasmiṃ ambavanai viharantassa yaibhuyyaina tayau pāpakā akusalā vitak‍kā samudācaranti, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmavitak‍kau, byāpādavitak‍kau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘acchariyaṃ vata bhau, abbhutaṃ vata bhau! saddhāya ca vatamhā agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitā; atha ca panimaihi  tīhi pāpakaihi akusalaihi vitak‍kaihi anvāsattā – kāmavitak‍kaina, byāpādavitak‍kaina, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaināti’’’|

‘‘aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā pañ‍ca dhammā paripak‍kāya saṃvattanti| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, maighiya, bhikkhu kalyāṇamittau hauti kalyāṇasahāyau kalyāṇasampavaṅkau| aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā ayaṃ paṭhamau dhammau paripak‍kāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhu sīlavā hauti, pātimaukkhasaṃvarasaṃvutau viharati ācāragaucarasampan‍nau aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu| aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā ayaṃ dutiyau dhammau paripak‍kāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, maighiya, yāyaṃ kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā  santuṭṭhikathā pavivaikakathā asaṃsaggakathā  vīriyārambhakathā sīlakathā samādhikathā pañ‍ñākathā vimuttikathā vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, aivarūpiyā kathāya nikāmalābhī hauti akicchalābhī akasiralābhī| aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā ayaṃ tatiyau dhammau paripak‍kāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau viharati akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya, kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadāya, thāmavā daḷhaparak‍kamau anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu| aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā ayaṃ catutthau dhammau paripak‍kāya saṃvattati|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhu pañ‍ñavā hauti udayatthagāminiyā pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau ariyāya nibbaidhikāya sammā dukkhakkhayagāminiyā| aparipak‍kāya, maighiya, caitauvimuttiyā ayaṃ pañ‍camau dhammau paripak‍kāya saṃvattati|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa – ‘sīlavā bhavissati…pai॰ … samādāya sikkhissati sikkhāpadaisu’’’|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa – ‘yāyaṃ kathā abhisal‍laikhikā caitauvivaraṇasappāyā, saiyyathidaṃ – appicchakathā…pai॰… vimuttiñāṇadassanakathā, aivarūpiyā kathāya nikāmalābhī bhavissati akicchalābhī akasiralābhī’’’|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa – ‘āraddhavīriyau viharissati…pai॰… anikkhittadhurau kusalaisu dhammaisu’’’|

‘‘kalyāṇamittassaitaṃ, maighiya, bhikkhunau pāṭikaṅkhaṃ kalyāṇasahāyassa kalyāṇasampavaṅkassa – ‘pañ‍ñavā bhavissati…pai॰… sammādukkhakkhayagāminiyā’’’|

‘‘taina  ca pana, maighiya, bhikkhunā imaisu pañ‍casu dhammaisu patiṭṭhāya cattārau dhammā uttari bhāvaitabbā – asubhā bhāvaitabbā rāgassa pahānāya, maittā bhāvaitabbā byāpādassa pahānāya, ānāpānassati bhāvaitabbā vitak‍kupacchaidāya, anic‍casañ‍ñā bhāvaitabbā asmimānasamugghātāya| anic‍casañ‍ñinau, maighiya, anattasañ‍ñā saṇṭhāti| anattasañ‍ñī asmimānasamugghātaṃ pāpuṇāti diṭṭhaiva dhammai nibbāna’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. nandakasuttaṃ

4. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā nandakau upaṭṭhānasālāyaṃ bhikkhū dhammiyā kathāya sandassaiti samādapaiti samuttaijaiti sampahaṃsaiti| atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yainupaṭṭhānasālā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai aṭṭhāsi kathāpariyausānaṃ āgamayamānau| atha khau bhagavā kathāpariyausānaṃ viditvā uk‍kāsaitvā aggaḷaṃ  ākauṭaisi| vivariṃsu khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau dvāraṃ|

atha  khau bhagavā upaṭṭhānasālaṃ pavisitvā pañ‍ñattāsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘dīghau khau tyāyaṃ, nandaka, dhammapariyāyau bhikkhūnaṃ paṭibhāsi| api mai piṭṭhi āgilāyati bahidvārakauṭṭhakai  ṭhitassa kathāpariyausānaṃ āgamayamānassā’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā nandakau sāraj‍jamānarūpau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau pana mayaṃ, bhantai, jānāma ‘bhagavā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitau’ti| sacai hi mayaṃ, bhantai, jānaiyyāma ‘bhagavā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai ṭhitau’ti, aittakampi ( ) (dhammaṃ) katthaci nau nappaṭibhāsaiyyā’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ sāraj‍jamānarūpaṃ viditvā āyasmantaṃ nandakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu, sādhu, nandaka! aitaṃ khau, nandaka, tumhākaṃ patirūpaṃ kulaputtānaṃ saddhāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitānaṃ, yaṃ tumhai dhammiyā kathāya san‍nisīdaiyyātha| san‍nipatitānaṃ vau, nandaka, dvayaṃ karaṇīyaṃ – dhammī vā kathā ariyau vā tuṇhībhāvau| a॰ ni॰ 8.71; 9.1 saddhau ca, nandaka, bhikkhu  hauti, nau ca sīlavā| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ  sīlavā cā’ti| yatau ca khau, nandaka, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti|

‘‘saddhau ca, nandaka, bhikkhu hauti sīlavā ca, nau ca lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissā’ti| yatau ca khau, nandaka, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti|

‘‘saddhau ca, nandaka, bhikkhu hauti sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa, na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina  aparipūrau hauti| saiyyathāpi, nandaka, pāṇakau catuppādakau assa| tassa aikau pādau aumakau lāmakau| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau assa| aivamaivaṃ khau, nandaka, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa, na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya| aivaṃ  sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāyā’’’ti|

‘‘yatau ca khau, nandaka, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hautī’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ pāvisi|

atha khau āyasmā nandakau acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘idāni, āvusau, bhagavā catūhi padaihi kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaitvā  uṭṭhāyāsanā vihāraṃ paviṭṭhau – ‘saddhau ca, nandaka, bhikkhu hauti, nau ca sīlavā| aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti| taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ – kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ sīlavā cā’ti| yatau ca khau nandaka bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hauti| saddhau ca nandaka bhikkhu hauti sīlavā ca, nau ca lābhī ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa…pai॰… lābhī  ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa, na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau  hauti| saiyyathāpi nandaka pāṇakau catuppādakau assa, tassa aikau pādau aumakau lāmakau, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau assa| aivamaivaṃ khau, nandaka, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca, lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa, na lābhī adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina aparipūrau hauti, taina taṃ aṅgaṃ paripūraitabbaṃ ‘kintāhaṃ saddhau ca assaṃ sīlavā ca, lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa, lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāyā’ti| yatau ca khau, nandaka, bhikkhu saddhau ca hauti sīlavā ca lābhī ca ajjhattaṃ caitausamādhissa lābhī ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya, aivaṃ sau tainaṅgaina paripūrau hautī’’ti|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, ānisaṃsā kālaina dhammassavanai kālaina dhammasākacchāya| katamai pañ‍ca? idhāvusau, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| yathā yathā, āvusau, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti, tathā tathā sau satthu piyau ca hauti manāpau ca garu ca bhāvanīyau ca| ayaṃ, āvusau, paṭhamau ānisaṃsau kālaina dhammassavanai kālaina dhammasākacchāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| yathā yathā, āvusau, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ…pai॰… brahmacariyaṃ  pakāsaiti, tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca hauti dhammappaṭisaṃvaidī ca| ayaṃ, āvusau, dutiyau ānisaṃsau kālaina dhammassavanai kālaina dhammasākacchāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| yathā yathā, āvusau, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ…pai॰… brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti, tathā tathā sau tasmiṃ dhammai gambhīraṃ atthapadaṃ pañ‍ñāya ativijjha passati| ayaṃ, āvusau, tatiyau ānisaṃsau kālaina dhammassavanai kālaina dhammasākacchāya|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ…pai॰… brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| yathā yathā, āvusau, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ…pai॰… brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti, tathā tathā naṃ sabrahmacārī uttari sambhāvainti – ‘addhā ayamāyasmā pattau vā paj‍jati vā’| ayaṃ, āvusau, catutthau ānisaṃsau kālaina dhammassavanai kālaina dhammasākacchāya|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti| yathā yathā, āvusau, bhikkhu bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti ādikalyāṇaṃ majjhaikalyāṇaṃ pariyausānakalyāṇaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ, kaivalaparipuṇṇaṃ parisuddhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ pakāsaiti, tattha yai khau bhikkhū saikhā appattamānasā anuttaraṃ yaugakkhaimaṃ patthayamānā viharanti, tai taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā vīriyaṃ ārabhanti appattassa pattiyā anadhigatassa  adhigamāya asacchikatassa sacchikiriyāya| yai pana tattha bhikkhū arahantau khīṇāsavā vusitavantau katakaraṇīyā auhitabhārā anuppattasadatthā parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanā sammadañ‍ñāvimuttā, tai taṃ dhammaṃ sutvā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāraṃyaiva  anuyuttā viharanti| ayaṃ, āvusau, pañ‍camau ānisaṃsau kālaina dhammassavanai kālaina dhammasākacchāya| imai khau, āvusau, pañ‍ca ānisaṃsā kālaina dhammassavanai kālaina dhammasākacchāyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. balasuttaṃ

5. ‘‘cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, balāni| katamāni cattāri? pañ‍ñābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, anavaj‍jabalaṃ, saṅgāhabalaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñābalaṃ? yai dhammā kusalā kusalasaṅkhātā yai dhammā akusalā akusalasaṅkhātā yai dhammā sāvaj‍jā sāvaj‍jasaṅkhātā yai dhammā anavaj‍jā anavaj‍jasaṅkhātā yai dhammā kaṇhā kaṇhasaṅkhātā yai dhammā suk‍kā suk‍kasaṅkhātā yai dhammā saivitabbā saivitabbasaṅkhātā yai dhammā asaivitabbā asaivitabbasaṅkhātā yai dhammā nālamariyā nālamariyasaṅkhātā yai dhammā alamariyā alamariyasaṅkhātā, tyāssa dhammā pañ‍ñāya vaudiṭṭhā haunti vaucaritā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñābalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vīriyabalaṃ? yai dhammā akusalā akusalasaṅkhātā yai dhammā sāvaj‍jā sāvaj‍jasaṅkhātā yai dhammā kaṇhā kaṇhasaṅkhātā yai dhammā asaivitabbā asaivitabbasaṅkhātā yai dhammā nālamariyā nālamariyasaṅkhātā, taisaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| yai dhammā kusalā kusalasaṅkhātā yai dhammā anavaj‍jā anavaj‍jasaṅkhātā yai dhammā suk‍kā suk‍kasaṅkhātā yai dhammā saivitabbā saivitabbasaṅkhātā  yai dhammā alamariyā alamariyasaṅkhātā, taisaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭilābhāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vīriyabalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anavaj‍jabalaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau anavaj‍jaina kāyakammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, anavaj‍jaina vacīkammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti, anavaj‍jaina manaukammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, anavaj‍jabalaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṅgāhabalaṃ? cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, saṅgahavatthūni – dānaṃ, paiyyavaj‍jaṃ, atthacariyā, samānattatā| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, dānānaṃ yadidaṃ dhammadānaṃ| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, paiyyavaj‍jānaṃ yadidaṃ atthikassa auhitasautassa punappunaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, atthacariyānaṃ yadidaṃ assaddhaṃ saddhāsampadāya samādapaiti nivaisaiti patiṭṭhāpaiti, dussīlaṃ sīlasampadāya… pai॰… macchariṃ cāgasampadāya…pai॰… duppañ‍ñaṃ pañ‍ñāsampadāya samādapaiti nivaisaiti patiṭṭhāpaiti| aitadaggaṃ, bhikkhavai, samānattatānaṃ yadidaṃ  sautāpan‍nau sautāpan‍nassa samānattau, sakadāgāmī sakadāgāmissa samānattau, anāgāmī anāgāmissa samānattau, arahā arahatau samānattau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, saṅgāhabalaṃ| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, cattāri balāni|

‘‘imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi balaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau pañ‍ca bhayāni samatik‍kantau hauti| katamāni pañ‍ca? ājīvikabhayaṃ, asilaukabhayaṃ, parisasāraj‍jabhayaṃ, maraṇabhayaṃ, duggatibhayaṃ| sa khau sau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘nāhaṃ ājīvikabhayassa bhāyāmi| kissāhaṃ ājīvikabhayassa bhāyissāmi? atthi mai cattāri balāni – pañ‍ñābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, anavaj‍jabalaṃ, saṅgāhabalaṃ| duppañ‍ñau khau ājīvikabhayassa  bhāyaiyya| kusītau ājīvikabhayassa bhāyaiyya| sāvaj‍jakāyakammantavacīkammantamanaukammantau ājīvikabhayassa bhāyaiyya| asaṅgāhakau ājīvikabhayassa bhāyaiyya| nāhaṃ asilaukabhayassa bhāyāmi…pai॰… nāhaṃ parisasāraj‍jabhayassa bhāyāmi…pai॰… nāhaṃ maraṇabhayassa bhāyāmi…pai॰… nāhaṃ duggatibhayassa bhāyāmi| kissāhaṃ duggatibhayassa bhāyissāmi? atthi mai cattāri balāni – pañ‍ñābalaṃ, vīriyabalaṃ, anavaj‍jabalaṃ, saṅgāhabalaṃ| duppañ‍ñau khau duggatibhayassa bhāyaiyya| kusītau duggatibhayassa bhāyaiyya| sāvaj‍jakāyakammantavacīkammantamanaukammantau duggatibhayassa bhāyaiyya| asaṅgāhakau duggatibhayassa bhāyaiyya| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, catūhi balaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni samatik‍kantau hautī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. saivanāsuttaṃ

6. tatra  khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi…pai॰… āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘puggalaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupi| cīvarampi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbaṃ – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi| piṇḍapātaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupi| saināsanampi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbaṃ – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampi| gāmanigamaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi  asaivitabbaupi| janapadapadaisaupi āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupi|

‘‘‘puggalaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā puggalaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai puggalaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti; yai ca khau mai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā tai ca kasiraina samudāgacchanti; yassa camhi atthāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau sau ca mai sāmañ‍ñatthau  na bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī’ti, taināvusau, puggalaina sau puggalau rattibhāgaṃ vā divasabhāgaṃ vā saṅkhāpi anāpucchā pak‍kamitabbaṃ nānubandhitabbau|

‘‘tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā puggalaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai puggalaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyanti; yai ca khau mai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā tai  ca appakasiraina samudāgacchanti; yassa camhi atthāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau sau ca mai sāmañ‍ñatthau na bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī’ti, taināvusau, puggalaina sau puggalau saṅkhāpi anāpucchā pak‍kamitabbaṃ nānubandhitabbau|

‘‘tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā puggalaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai puggalaṃ saivatau akusalā  dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti; yai ca khau mai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā tai ca kasiraina samudāgacchanti; yassa camhi atthāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau sau ca mai sāmañ‍ñatthau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī’ti, taināvusau, puggalaina sau puggalau saṅkhāpi anubandhitabbau na pak‍kamitabbaṃ|

‘‘tattha  yaṃ jañ‍ñā puggalaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai puggalaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti; yai ca khau mai pabbajitaina jīvitaparikkhārā samudānaitabbā cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārā tai ca appakasiraina samudāgacchanti; yassa camhi atthāya agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajitau sau ca mai sāmañ‍ñatthau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchatī’ti, taināvusau, puggalaina sau puggalau yāvajīvaṃ anubandhitabbau na pak‍kamitabbaṃ api panuj‍jamānaina paṇuj‍jamānaina (?)| ‘puggalaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘cīvarampi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbaṃ – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti  khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā cīvaraṃ – ‘idaṃ khau mai cīvaraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ na saivitabbaṃ| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā cīvaraṃ – ‘idaṃ khau mai cīvaraṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpaṃ cīvaraṃ saivitabbaṃ| ‘cīvarampi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbaṃ – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘piṇḍapātaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā piṇḍapātaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai piṇḍapātaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpau piṇḍapātau na saivitabbau| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā piṇḍapātaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai piṇḍapātaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpau piṇḍapātau saivitabbau| ‘piṇḍapātaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘saināsanampi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbaṃ – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā saināsanaṃ – ‘‘idaṃ khau mai saināsanaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpaṃ saināsanaṃ na  saivitabbaṃ| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā saināsanaṃ – ‘idaṃ khau mai saināsanaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpaṃ saināsanaṃ saivitabbaṃ| ‘saināsanampi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbaṃ – saivitabbampi asaivitabbampī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘gāmanigamaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā gāmanigamaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai gāmanigamaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpau gāmanigamau na saivitabbau| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā gāmanigamaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau, mai gāmanigamaṃ  saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpau gāmanigamau saivitabbau| ‘gāmanigamaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘janapadapadaisaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā janapadapadaisaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai janapadapadaisaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhanti, kusalā dhammā parihāyantī’ti, aivarūpau janapadapadaisau na saivitabbau| tattha yaṃ jañ‍ñā janapadapadaisaṃ – ‘imaṃ khau mai janapadapadaisaṃ saivatau akusalā dhammā parihāyanti, kusalā dhammā abhivaḍḍhantī’ti, aivarūpau janapadapadaisau saivitabbau| ‘janapadapadaisaupi, āvusau, duvidhaina  vaiditabbau – saivitabbaupi asaivitabbaupī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vutta’’nti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sutavāsuttaṃ

7. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau sutavā paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sutavā paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ bhagavā idhaiva rājagahai viharāmi giribbajai| tatra mai, bhantai, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yau sau, sutavā  sutava (syā॰), bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau  anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñāvimuttau, abhabbau sau pañ‍ca ṭhānāni ajjhācarituṃ – abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātuṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sampajānamusā sampajānaṃ musā (ka॰ sī॰) bhāsituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu san‍nidhikārakaṃ kāmai paribhuñ‍jituṃ saiyyathāpi pubbai agāriyabhūtau’ti| kac‍ci maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sussutaṃ suggahitaṃ sumanasikataṃ sūpadhārita’’nti?

‘‘taggha tai aitaṃ, sutavā, sussutaṃ suggahitaṃ sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ| pubbai cāhaṃ, sutavā, aitarahi ca aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘yau sau bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñāvimuttau, abhabbau  sau nava ṭhānāni ajjhācarituṃ  – abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātuṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sampajānamusā bhāsituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu san‍nidhikārakaṃ kāmai paribhuñ‍jituṃ saiyyathāpi pubbai agāriyabhūtau, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu chandāgatiṃ gantuṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu dausāgatiṃ gantuṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu mauhāgatiṃ gantuṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu bhayāgatiṃ  gantuṃ’| pubbai cāhaṃ, sutavā, aitarahi ca aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘yau sau bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñāvimuttau, abhabbau sau imāni nava ṭhānāni ajjhācaritu’’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. sajjhasuttaṃ

8. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati gijjhakūṭai pabbatai| atha khau sajjhau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ  nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sajjhau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, bhantai, samayaṃ bhagavā idhaiva rājagahai viharāmi giribbajai| tatra mai, bhantai, bhagavatau sammukhā sutaṃ sammukhā paṭiggahitaṃ – ‘yau sau, sajjha, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñāvimuttau, abhabbau sau pañ‍ca ṭhānāni ajjhācarituṃ – abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca  pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādātuṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sampajānamusā bhāsituṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu san‍nidhikārakaṃ kāmai paribhuñ‍jituṃ saiyyathāpi pubbai agāriyabhūtau’ti| kac‍ci maitaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau sussutaṃ suggahitaṃ sumanasikataṃ sūpadhārita’’nti?

‘‘taggha  tai aitaṃ, sajjha, sussutaṃ suggahitaṃ sumanasikataṃ sūpadhāritaṃ| pubbai cāhaṃ, sajjha, aitarahi ca aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘yau sau bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñāvimuttau, abhabbau sau nava ṭhānāni ajjhācarituṃ – abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaituṃ…pai॰… abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu san‍nidhikārakaṃ kāmai paribhuñ‍jituṃ saiyyathāpi pubbai agāriyabhūtau, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu buddhaṃ pac‍cakkhātuṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu dhammaṃ pac‍cakkhātuṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu saṅghaṃ pac‍cakkhātuṃ, abhabbau khīṇāsavau bhikkhu sikkhaṃ pac‍cakkhātuṃ’| pubbai cāhaṃ, sajjha, aitarahi ca aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘yau sau bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñāvimuttau, abhabbau sau imāni nava ṭhānāni ajjhācaritu’’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. puggalasuttaṃ

9. ‘‘navayimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmiṃ| katamai  nava? arahā, arahattāya paṭipan‍nau, anāgāmī, anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, sakadāgāmī, sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, sautāpan‍nau, sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, puthuj‍janau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, nava puggalā santau saṃvij‍jamānā laukasmi’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. āhunaiyyasuttaṃ

10. ‘‘navayimai, bhikkhavai, puggalā āhunaiyyā pāhunaiyyā dakkhiṇaiyyā añ‍jalikaraṇīyā anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassa| katamai nava? arahā, arahattāya paṭipan‍nau, anāgāmī, anāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, sakadāgāmī, sakadāgāmiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, sautāpan‍nau, sautāpattiphalasacchikiriyāya paṭipan‍nau, gautrabhū – imai khau, bhikkhavai, nava puggalā āhunaiyyā…pai॰… anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sambaudhivaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ  –

sambaudhi nissayau caiva, maighiya nandakaṃ balaṃ|

saivanā sutavā sajjhau, puggalau āhunaiyyaina cāti||


2. sīhanādavaggau

1. sīhanādasuttaṃ

11. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vutthau  mai, bhantai, sāvatthiyaṃ vassāvāsau| icchāmahaṃ, bhantai, janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamitu’’nti| ‘‘yassadāni tvaṃ, sāriputta, kālaṃ mañ‍ñasī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu acirapak‍kantai āyasmantai sāriputtai bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘āyasmā maṃ, bhantai, sāriputtau āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’’ti| atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi  tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina sāriputtaṃ āmantaihi – ‘satthā taṃ, āvusau sāriputta, āmantaitī’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau sāriputta, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā sāriputtau tassa bhikkhunau pac‍cassausi|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā ca mahāmauggal‍lānau mahāmauggalānau (ka॰) āyasmā ca ānandau avāpuraṇaṃ apāpuraṇaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) ādāya vihārai āhiṇḍanti vihāraina vihāraṃ anvāhiṇḍanti (sī॰ pī॰), vihāraṃ āhiṇḍanti (syā॰) – ‘‘abhik‍kamathāyasmantau, abhik‍kamathāyasmantau! idānāyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau sammukhā sīhanādaṃ nadissatī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘idha tai, sāriputta, añ‍ñatarau sabrahmacārī khīyanadhammaṃ āpan‍nau – ‘āyasmā maṃ, bhantai, sāriputtau āsaj‍ja  appaṭinissaj‍jacārikaṃ pak‍kantau’’’ti|

‘‘yassa  nūna, bhantai, kāyai kāyagatāsati anupaṭṭhitā assa, sau idha añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, pathaviyaṃ sucimpi nikkhipanti asucimpi nikkhipanti gūthagatampi nikkhipanti muttagatampi nikkhipanti khaiḷagatampi nikkhipanti pubbagatampi nikkhipanti lauhitagatampi nikkhipanti, na ca taina pathavī aṭṭīyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā; aivamaivaṃ  khau ahaṃ, bhantai, pathavīsamaina caitasā viharāmi vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| yassa nūna, bhantai, kāyai kāyagatāsati anupaṭṭhitā assa, sau idha añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, āpasmiṃ sucimpi dhauvanti asucimpi dhauvanti gūthagatampi… muttagatampi… khaiḷagatampi… pubbagatampi… lauhitagatampi dhauvanti, na ca taina āpau aṭṭīyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, āpausamaina caitasā viharāmi vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina  avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| yassa nūna, bhantai, kāyai kāyagatāsati anupaṭṭhitā assa, sau idha añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, taijau sucimpi ḍahati asucimpi ḍahati gūthagatampi… muttagatampi… khaiḷagatampi… pubbagatampi… lauhitagatampi ḍahati, na ca taina taijau aṭṭīyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, taijausamaina caitasā viharāmi vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| yassa nūna, bhantai, kāyai kāyagatāsati anupaṭṭhitā assa, sau idha añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, vāyau sucimpi upavāyati asucimpi upavāyati gūthagatampi… muttagatampi… khaiḷagatampi… pubbagatampi… lauhitagatampi upavāyati, na ca taina vāyau aṭṭīyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, vāyausamaina caitasā viharāmi vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| yassa nūna, bhantai, kāyai kāyagatāsati  anupaṭṭhitā assa, sau idha añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, rajauharaṇaṃ sucimpi puñchati asucimpi puñchati gūthagatampi… muttagatampi… khaiḷagatampi… pubbagatampi… lauhitagatampi puñchati, na ca taina rajauharaṇaṃ aṭṭīyati vā harāyati vā jigucchati vā; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, rajauharaṇasamaina caitasā viharāmi vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| yassa nūna, bhantai, kāyai kāyagatāsati anupaṭṭhitā assa, sau idha añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, caṇḍālakumārakau vā caṇḍālakumārikā vā kaḷaupihatthau nantakavāsī gāmaṃ vā nigamaṃ vā pavisantau nīcacittaṃyaiva upaṭṭhapaitvā  pavisati; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, caṇḍālakumārakacaṇḍālakumārikāsamaina caitasā viharāmi vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| yassa nūna, bhantai, kāyai kāyagatāsati  anupaṭṭhitā assa, sau idha añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, usabhau chin‍navisāṇau sūratau sudantau suvinītau rathiyāya rathiyaṃ siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ anvāhiṇḍantau na kiñ‍ci hiṃsati pādaina vā visāṇaina vā; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, usabhachin‍navisāṇasamaina caitasā viharāmi vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina| yassa nūna, bhantai, kāyai kāyagatāsati anupaṭṭhitā assa, sau idha añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanakajātikau sīsaṃnhātau ahikuṇapaina vā kuk‍kurakuṇapaina  vā manussakuṇapaina vā kaṇṭhai āsattaina aṭṭīyaiyya harāyaiyya jigucchaiyya; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, iminā pūtikāyaina aṭṭīyāmi harāyāmi jigucchāmi| yassa nūna, bhantai, kāyai kāyagatāsati anupaṭṭhitā assa, sau idha añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhantai, purisau maidakathālikaṃ pariharaiyya chiddāvachiddaṃ uggharantaṃ paggharantaṃ; aivamaivaṃ khau ahaṃ, bhantai, imaṃ kāyaṃ pariharāmi chiddāvachiddaṃ  uggharantaṃ paggharantaṃ| yassa nūna, bhantai, kāyai kāyagatāsati anupaṭṭhitā assa, sau idha añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyyā’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā bhagavatau pādaisu sirasā nipatitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘ac‍cayau maṃ, bhantai, ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yau ahaṃ āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ asatā tucchā musā abhūtaina abbhācikkhiṃ| tassa mai, bhantai, bhagavā ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau paṭiggaṇhatu āyatiṃ saṃvarāyā’’ti| ‘‘taggha taṃ tvaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), bhikkhu, ac‍cayau ac‍cagamā yathābālaṃ yathāmūḷhaṃ yathāakusalaṃ, yau tvaṃ sāriputtaṃ asatā tucchā musā abhūtaina abbhācikkhi| yatau ca khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ  paṭikarausi, taṃ tai mayaṃ paṭiggaṇhāma| vuḍḍhihaisā, bhikkhu, ariyassa vinayai yau ac‍cayaṃ ac‍cayatau disvā yathādhammaṃ paṭikarauti āyatiṃ saṃvaraṃ āpaj‍jatī’’ti|

atha  khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘khama, sāriputta, imassa maughapurisassa, purā tassa tatthaiva sattadhā muddhā phalatī’’ti phalissatīti (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) aṭṭhakathāsu pana ‘‘phalatīti’’ itvaiva dissati| ‘‘khamāmahaṃ, bhantai, tassa āyasmatau sacai maṃ sau āyasmā aivamāha – ‘khamatu ca mai sau āyasmā’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. saupādisaisasuttaṃ

12. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya  sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| atha khau āyasmatau sāriputtassa aitadahausi – ‘‘atippagau khau tāva sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya carituṃ, yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi|

taina  khau pana samayaina taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, saupādisaisau kālaṃ karauti, sabbau sau aparimuttau nirayā aparimuttau tiracchānayauniyā aparimuttau paittivisayā aparimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandi nappaṭik‍kausi| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi – ‘‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī’’ti| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ  piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisiṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, aitadahausi – ‘atippagau khau tāva sāvatthiyaṃ  piṇḍāya carituṃ; yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’nti| atha khau ahaṃ, bhantai, yaina añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ ārāmau tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā taihi añ‍ñatitthiyaihi paribbājakaihi saddhiṃ sammaudiṃ| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| taina khau pana samayaina taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ san‍nisin‍nānaṃ san‍nipatitānaṃ ayamantarākathā udapādi – ‘yau hi kauci, āvusau, saupādisaisau kālaṃ karauti, sabbau sau aparimuttau nirayā aparimuttau tiracchānayauniyā aparimuttau paittivisayā aparimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā’ti| atha khau ahaṃ, bhantai, taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ bhāsitaṃ naiva abhinandiṃ nappaṭik‍kausiṃ| anabhinanditvā appaṭik‍kausitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃ – ‘bhagavatau santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthaṃ ājānissāmī’’’ti|

‘‘kai ca kaici (syā॰ pī॰), tai ca (ka॰), sāriputta, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā bālā abyattā, kai ca kaici (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 6.44 pāḷiyā saṃsandaitabbaṃ saupādisaisaṃ vā ‘saupādisaisau’ti jānissanti, anupādisaisaṃ vā ‘anupādisaisau’ti jānissanti’’!

‘‘navayimai, sāriputta, puggalā saupādisaisā kālaṃ kurumānā parimuttā nirayā parimuttā tiracchānayauniyā parimuttā paittivisayā parimuttā apāyaduggativinipātā| katamai nava? idha, sāriputta, aikac‍cau puggalau sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti, samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī, pañ‍ñāya mattasau kārī| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā antarāparinibbāyī hauti| ayaṃ, sāriputta, paṭhamau puggalau saupādisaisau kālaṃ kurumānau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā  parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti, samādhismiṃ paripūrakārī, pañ‍ñāya mattasau kārī| sau pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti…pai॰… uddhaṃsautau hauti akaniṭṭhagāmī| ayaṃ, sāriputta, pañ‍camau puggalau saupādisaisau kālaṃ kurumānau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti, samādhismiṃ mattasau kārī, pañ‍ñāya mattasau kārī| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā rāgadausamauhānaṃ tanuttā sakadāgāmī hauti, sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| ayaṃ, sāriputta, chaṭṭhau puggalau saupādisaisau kālaṃ kurumānau parimuttau nirayā…pai॰… parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti, samādhismiṃ mattasau kārī, pañ‍ñāya mattasau kārī| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aikabījī hauti, aikaṃyaiva mānusakaṃ bhavaṃ nibbattaitvā dukkhassantaṃ  karauti| ayaṃ, sāriputta, sattamau puggalau saupādisaisau  kālaṃ kurumānau parimuttau nirayā…pai॰… parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti, samādhismiṃ mattasau kārī, pañ‍ñāya mattasau kārī| sau tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā kaulaṃkaulau hauti, dvai vā tīṇi vā kulāni sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| ayaṃ, sāriputta, aṭṭhamau puggalau saupādisaisau kālaṃ kurumānau parimuttau nirayā…pai॰… parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, sāriputta, idhaikac‍cau puggalau sīlaisu paripūrakārī hauti, samādhismiṃ mattasau kārī, pañ‍ñāya mattasau kārī| sau tiṇṇaṃ  saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā sattakkhattuparamau hauti, sattakkhattuparamaṃ daivai ca manussai ca sandhāvitvā saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti| ayaṃ, sāriputta, navamau puggalau saupādisaisau kālaṃ kurumānau parimuttau nirayā parimuttau tiracchānayauniyā parimuttau paittivisayā parimuttau apāyaduggativinipātā|

‘‘kai ca, sāriputta, añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā bālā abyattā, kai ca saupādisaisaṃ vā ‘saupādisaisau’ti jānissanti, anupādisaisaṃ vā ‘anupādisaisau’ti jānissanti! imai khau, sāriputta, nava puggalā saupādisaisā kālaṃ kurumānā parimuttā nirayā parimuttā tiracchānayauniyā parimuttā paittivisayā parimuttā apāyaduggativinipātā| na tāvāyaṃ, sāriputta, dhammapariyāyau paṭibhāsi bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunīnaṃ upāsakānaṃ upāsikānaṃ| taṃ kissa  haitu? māyimaṃ dhammapariyāyaṃ sutvā pamādaṃ āhariṃsūti āhariṃsu (sī॰ pī॰)| api  ca mayā api cāyaṃ (?), sāriputta, dhammapariyāyau pañhādhippāyaina bhāsitau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kauṭṭhikasuttaṃ

13. atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau mahākauṭṭhitau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, ‘yaṃ kammaṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ, taṃ mai kammaṃ samparāyavaidanīyaṃ hautū’ti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, ‘yaṃ kammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ sukhavaidaniyaṃ (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.8 passitabbaṃ, taṃ mai kammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ dukkhavaidaniyaṃ (ka॰) hautū’ti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, ‘yaṃ kammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ sukhavaidaniyaṃ (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.8 passitabbaṃ, taṃ mai kammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ dukkhavaidaniyaṃ (ka॰) hautū’ti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ  panāvusau, sāriputta, ‘yaṃ kammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ, taṃ mai kammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ hautū’ti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, ‘yaṃ kammaṃ paripak‍kavaidanīyaṃ, taṃ mai kammaṃ aparipak‍kavaidanīyaṃ hautū’ti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘nau  hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, ‘yaṃ kammaṃ aparipak‍kavaidanīyaṃ, taṃ mai kammaṃ paripak‍kavaidanīyaṃ hautū’ti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, ‘yaṃ kammaṃ bahuvaidanīyaṃ, taṃ mai kammaṃ appavaidanīyaṃ hautū’ti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, ‘yaṃ kammaṃ appavaidanīyaṃ, taṃ mai kammaṃ bahuvaidanīyaṃ hautū’ti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, ‘yaṃ kammaṃ vaidanīyaṃ, taṃ mai kammaṃ avaidanīyaṃ hautū’ti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, ‘yaṃ kammaṃ avaidanīyaṃ, taṃ mai kammaṃ vaidanīyaṃ hautū’ti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti? ‘‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, yaṃ kammaṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ taṃ mai kammaṃ samparāyavaidanīyaṃ hautūti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, yaṃ kammaṃ samparāyavaidanīyaṃ taṃ mai kammaṃ diṭṭhadhammavaidanīyaṃ hautūti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, yaṃ kammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ taṃ mai kammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ hautūti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, yaṃ kammaṃ dukkhavaidanīyaṃ taṃ mai kammaṃ sukhavaidanīyaṃ hautūti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, iti  puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, yaṃ kammaṃ paripak‍kavaidanīyaṃ taṃ mai kammaṃ aparipak‍kavaidanīyaṃ hautūti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, yaṃ kammaṃ aparipak‍kavaidanīyaṃ taṃ mai kammaṃ paripak‍kavaidanīyaṃ hautūti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ  vussatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, yaṃ kammaṃ bahuvaidanīyaṃ taṃ mai kammaṃ appavaidanīyaṃ hautūti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, yaṃ kammaṃ appavaidanīyaṃ taṃ mai kammaṃ bahuvaidanīyaṃ hautūti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu khau, āvusau sāriputta, yaṃ kammaṃ vaidanīyaṃ taṃ mai kammaṃ avaidanīyaṃ hautūti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi|

‘‘‘kiṃ panāvusau sāriputta, yaṃ kammaṃ avaidanīyaṃ taṃ mai kammaṃ vaidanīyaṃ hautūti, aitassa atthāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nau hidaṃ, āvusau’ti vadaisi| atha kimatthaṃ carahāvusau, bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti?

‘‘yaṃ khvassa yaṃ khau (ka॰), āvusau, añ‍ñātaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ appattaṃ asacchikataṃ anabhisamaitaṃ, tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya pattiyā sacchikiriyāya abhisamayāya bhagavati  brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti vussati (syā॰)| (‘‘kiṃ panassāvusau, añ‍ñātaṃ  adiṭṭhaṃ appattaṃ asacchikataṃ anabhisamaitaṃ, yassa ñāṇāya dassanāya pattiyā sacchikiriyāya abhisamayāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti?) ( ) syā॰ ka॰ pautthakaisu natthi ‘‘‘idaṃ dukkha’nti khvassa khau yaṃ (ka॰), āvusau, añ‍ñātaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ appattaṃ asacchikataṃ anabhisamaitaṃ| tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya pattiyā sacchikiriyāya abhisamayāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati| ayaṃ  ‘dukkhasamudayau’ti khvassa, āvusau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti khvassa, āvusau…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti khvassa, āvusau, añ‍ñātaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ appattaṃ asacchikataṃ anabhisamaitaṃ| tassa ñāṇāya dassanāya pattiyā sacchikiriyāya abhisamayāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussati| idaṃ khvassa iti khau yaṃ (ka॰), āvusau, añ‍ñātaṃ adiṭṭhaṃ appattaṃ asacchikataṃ anabhisamaitaṃ| tassa yassa (?) ñāṇāya dassanāya pattiyā sacchikiriyāya abhisamayāya bhagavati brahmacariyaṃ vussatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samiddhisuttaṃ

14. atha khau āyasmā samiddhi yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ samiddhiṃ āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca – ‘‘kimārammaṇā, samiddhi, purisassa saṅkappavitak‍kā uppaj‍jantī’’ti? ‘‘nāmarūpārammaṇā, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘tai pana, samiddhi, k‍va nānattaṃ gacchantī’’ti? ‘‘dhātūsu, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃsamudayā’’ti? ‘‘phassasamudayā, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃsamausaraṇā’’ti? ‘‘vaidanāsamausaraṇā, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃpamukhā’’ti? ‘‘samādhippamukhā, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃadhipataiyyā’’ti? ‘‘satādhipataiyyā, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃuttarā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍ñuttarā, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃsārā’’ti? ‘‘vimuttisārā, bhantai’’ti| ‘‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃaugadhā’’ti? ‘‘amataugadhā, bhantai’’ti|

‘‘‘kimārammaṇā, samiddhi, purisassa saṅkappavitak‍kā uppaj‍jantī’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘nāmarūpārammaṇā, bhantai’ti vadaisi| ‘tai pana, samiddhi, k‍va  nānattaṃ gacchantī’ti, iti  puṭṭhau samānau ‘dhātūsu, bhantai’ti vadaisi| ‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃsamudayā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘phassasamudayā, bhantai’ti  vadaisi| ‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃsamausaraṇā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘vaidanāsamausaraṇā, bhantai’ti vadaisi| ‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃpamukhā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘samādhippamukhā, bhantai’ti vadaisi| ‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃadhipataiyyā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘satādhipataiyyā, bhantai’ti vadaisi| ‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃuttarā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘pañ‍ñuttarā, bhantai’ti vadaisi| ‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃsārā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘vimuttisārā, bhantai’ti vadaisi| ‘tai pana, samiddhi, kiṃaugadhā’ti, iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘amataugadhā, bhantai’ti vadaisi| sādhu sādhu, samiddhi! sādhu khau tvaṃ, samiddhi, puṭṭhau pañhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaisi, taina ca mā mañ‍ñī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. gaṇḍasuttaṃ

15. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gaṇḍau anaikavassagaṇikau| tassassu gaṇḍassa nava vaṇamukhāni nava abhaidanamukhāni| tatau yaṃ kiñ‍ci paggharaiyya – asuciyaiva paggharaiyya, duggandhaṃyaiva paggharaiyya, jaigucchiyaṃyaiva jaigucchiyaiva (ka॰) paggharaiyya; yaṃ kiñ‍ci pasavaiyya – asuciyaiva pasavaiyya, duggandhaṃyaiva pasavaiyya, jaigucchiyaṃyaiva pasavaiyya|

‘‘gaṇḍauti khau, bhikkhavai, imassaitaṃ cātumahābhūtikassa cātummahābhūtikassa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) kāyassa adhivacanaṃ mātāpaittikasambhavassa audanakummāsūpacayassa anic‍cucchādanaparimaddanabhaidanaviddhaṃsanadhammassa| tassassu gaṇḍassa nava vaṇamukhāni nava abhaidanamukhāni| tatau yaṃ kiñ‍ci paggharati – asuciyaiva paggharati, duggandhaṃyaiva paggharati, jaigucchiyaṃyaiva  paggharati; yaṃ kiñ‍ci pasavati  – asuciyaiva  pasavati, duggandhaṃyaiva pasavati, jaigucchiyaṃyaiva pasavati| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, imasmiṃ kāyai nibbindathā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

16. ‘‘navayimā, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā| katamā nava? asubhasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā paṭik‍kūlasañ‍ñā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā anabhiratisañ‍ñā (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 5.121-122, anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, nava sañ‍ñā, bhāvitā bahulīkatā mahapphalā haunti mahānisaṃsā amataugadhā amatapariyausānā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. kulasuttaṃ

17. ‘‘navahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā nālaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā vā nālaṃ nisīdituṃ| katamaihi navahi? na manāpaina pac‍cuṭṭhainti, na manāpaina abhivādainti, na manāpaina āsanaṃ dainti, santamassa pariguhanti, bahukampi thaukaṃ dainti, paṇītampi lūkhaṃ dainti, asak‍kac‍caṃ dainti nau sak‍kac‍caṃ, na upanisīdanti dhammassavanāya, bhāsitamassa na sussūsanti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, navahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā nālaṃ upagantuṃ upagantvā vā nālaṃ nisīdituṃ|

‘‘navahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā alaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā vā alaṃ nisīdituṃ| katamaihi navahi? manāpaina pac‍cuṭṭhainti, manāpaina abhivādainti, manāpaina āsanaṃ dainti, santamassa  na pariguhanti, bahukampi  bahukaṃ dainti, paṇītampi paṇītaṃ dainti, sak‍kac‍caṃ dainti nau asak‍kac‍caṃ, upanisīdanti dhammassavanāya, bhāsitamassa sussūsanti| imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, navahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ kulaṃ anupagantvā vā alaṃ upagantuṃ, upagantvā vā alaṃ nisīditu’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. navaṅgupausathasuttaṃ

18. ‘‘navahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau upausathau upavutthau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau| kathaṃ upavutthau ca, bhikkhavai, navahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau upausathau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratā nihitadaṇḍā nihitasatthā laj‍jī  dayāpan‍nā sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampinau viharanti; ahampaj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ pāṇātipātaṃ pahāya pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau nihitadaṇḍau nihitasatthau laj‍jī dayāpan‍nau sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī viharāmi| imināpaṅgaina imināpi aṅgaina (ka॰ sī॰) arahataṃ anukaraumi; upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā paṭhamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti…pai॰ …|

‘‘‘yāvajīvaṃ arahantau uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratā nīcasaiyyaṃ kappainti  – mañ‍cakai vā tiṇasanthārakai vā; ahampaj‍ja imañ‍ca rattiṃ imañ‍ca divasaṃ uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanaṃ pahāya uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanā paṭiviratau nīcasaiyyaṃ kappaimi – mañ‍cakai vā tiṇasanthārakai vā| imināpaṅgaina arahataṃ  anukaraumi; upausathau ca mai upavutthau bhavissatī’ti| iminā aṭṭhamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘maittāsahagataina caitasā aikaṃ disaṃ pharitvā viharati, tathā dutiyaṃ tathā tatiyaṃ tathā catutthaṃ| iti uddhamadhau tiriyaṃ sabbadhi sabbattatāya sabbāvantaṃ laukaṃ maittāsahagataina caitasā vipulaina mahaggataina appamāṇaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina abyāpajjhaina (ka॰), abyābajjhaina (?) pharitvā viharati| iminā navamaina aṅgaina saman‍nāgatau hauti| aivaṃ upavutthau khau, bhikkhavai, navahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau upausathau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau mahājutikau mahāvipphārau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. daivatāsuttaṃ

19. ‘‘imañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rattiṃ sambahulā daivatā abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ jaitavanaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā  aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, bhikkhavai, tā daivatā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘upasaṅkamiṃsu nau, bhantai, pubbai manussabhūtānaṃ pabbajitā agārāni| tai mayaṃ, bhantai, pac‍cuṭṭhimha, nau ca khau abhivādimha| tā mayaṃ, bhantai, aparipuṇṇakammantā vippaṭisāriniyau pac‍cānutāpiniyau hīnaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nā’’’ti|

‘‘aparāpi  maṃ, bhikkhavai, sambahulā daivatā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘upasaṅkamiṃsu nau, bhantai, pubbai manussabhūtānaṃ pabbajitā agārāni| tai mayaṃ, bhantai, pac‍cuṭṭhimha abhivādimha pac‍cuṭṭhimha ca abhivādimha ca (syā॰), nau ca taisaṃ āsanaṃ adamha| tā mayaṃ, bhantai, aparipuṇṇakammantā vippaṭisāriniyau pac‍cānutāpiniyau hīnaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nā’’’ti|

‘‘aparāpi maṃ, bhikkhavai, sambahulā daivatā upasaṅkamitvā  aitadavaucuṃ – ‘upasaṅkamiṃsu nau, bhantai, pubbai manussabhūtānaṃ pabbajitā agārāni| tai mayaṃ, bhantai, pac‍cuṭṭhimha abhivādimha pac‍cuṭṭhimha ca abhivādimha ca (syā॰) āsanaṃ āsanañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰) adamha, nau ca khau yathāsatti yathābalaṃ saṃvibhajimha…pai॰… yathāsatti yathābalaṃ yathābalaṃ ca (?) saṃvibhajimha, nau ca khau upanisīdimha dhammassavanāya…pai॰… upanisīdimha upanisīdimha ca (syā॰) dhammassavanāya, nau ca khau auhitasautā dhammaṃ suṇimha…pai॰… auhitasautā ca dhammaṃ suṇimha, nau ca khau sutvā dhammaṃ dhārayimha…pai॰… sutvā ca dhammaṃ dhārayimha, nau ca khau dhātānaṃ dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhimha…pai॰… dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhimha, nau ca khau atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammaṃ paṭipaj‍jimha| tā mayaṃ, bhantai, aparipuṇṇakammantā vippaṭisāriniyau pac‍cānutāpiniyau hīnaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nā’’’ti|

‘‘aparāpi maṃ, bhikkhavai, sambahulā daivatā upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ – ‘upasaṅkamiṃsu nau, bhantai, pubbai manussabhūtānaṃ pabbajitā agārāni| tai mayaṃ, bhantai, pac‍cuṭṭhimha abhivādimha pac‍cuṭṭhimha ca abhivādimha ca (syā॰), āsanaṃ āsanañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰) adamha, yathāsatti  yathābalaṃ yathābalaṃ ca (?) saṃvibhajimha, upanisīdimha upanisīdimha ca (syā॰) dhammassavanāya, auhitasautā ca dhammaṃ suṇimha, sutvā ca dhammaṃ dhārayimha, dhātānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ atthaṃ upaparikkhimha, atthamañ‍ñāya dhammamañ‍ñāya dhammānudhammaṃ dhammānudhammañ‍ca (?) paṭipaj‍jimha| tā mayaṃ, bhantai, paripuṇṇakammantā avippaṭisāriniyau apac‍cānutāpiniyau paṇītaṃ kāyaṃ upapan‍nā’ti| aitāni, bhikkhavai, rukkhamūlāni aitāni suñ‍ñāgārāni| jhāyatha, bhikkhavai, mā pamādattha, mā pacchā vippaṭisārinau ahuvattha saiyyathāpi tā purimikā daivatā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. vailāmasuttaṃ

20. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘api nu tai, gahapati, kulai dānaṃ dīyatī’’ti? ‘‘dīyati mai, bhantai, kulai dānaṃ; tañ‍ca khau lūkhaṃ kaṇājakaṃ biḷaṅgadutiya’’nti| ‘‘lūkhañ‍caipi lūkhaṃ vāpi (syā॰), lūkhañ‍cāpi (ka॰), gahapati, dānaṃ daiti paṇītaṃ vā; tañ‍ca asak‍kac‍caṃ daiti, acittīkatvā acittiṃ katvā (ka॰), apacittiṃ katvā (syā॰), acittikatvā (pī॰) daiti, asahatthā daiti, apaviddhaṃ apaviṭṭhaṃ (syā॰) daiti, anāgamanadiṭṭhikau daiti| yattha yattha tassa tassa dānassa vipākau nibbattati, na uḷārāya bhattabhaugāya cittaṃ namati, na uḷārāya vatthabhaugāya cittaṃ namati, na uḷārāya yānabhaugāya cittaṃ namati, na uḷāraisu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu bhaugāya cittaṃ namati| yaipissa tai haunti  puttāti vā dārāti vā dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā, taipi na sussūsanti na sautaṃ audahanti na añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapainti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ aivañ‍caitaṃ (syā॰ ka॰), gahapati, hauti asak‍kac‍caṃ katānaṃ kammānaṃ vipākau’’|

‘‘lūkhañ‍caipi, gahapati, dānaṃ daiti paṇītaṃ vā; tañ‍ca sak‍kac‍caṃ daiti, cittīkatvā daiti, sahatthā daiti, anapaviddhaṃ daiti, āgamanadiṭṭhikau daiti| yattha yattha tassa tassa dānassa vipākau nibbattati, uḷārāya bhattabhaugāya cittaṃ namati, uḷārāya vatthabhaugāya cittaṃ namati, uḷārāya yānabhaugāya cittaṃ namati, uḷāraisu pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu bhaugāya cittaṃ namati| yaipissa tai haunti puttāti  vā dārāti vā dāsāti vā paissāti vā kammakarāti vā, taipi sussūsanti sautaṃ audahanti añ‍ñā cittaṃ upaṭṭhapainti| taṃ kissa haitu? aivañhaitaṃ, gahapati, hauti sak‍kac‍caṃ katānaṃ kammānaṃ vipākau|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, gahapati, vailāmau nāma brāhmaṇau ahausi| sau aivarūpaṃ dānaṃ adāsi mahādānaṃ| caturāsīti suvaṇṇapātisahassāni adāsi rūpiyapūrāni, caturāsīti rūpiyapātisahassāni adāsi suvaṇṇapūrāni, caturāsīti kaṃsapātisahassāni adāsi hirañ‍ñapūrāni, caturāsīti hatthisahassāni adāsi sauvaṇṇālaṅkārāni sauvaṇṇadhajāni haimajālappaṭicchan‍nāni haimajālasañchan‍nāni (sī॰ pī॰), caturāsīti rathasahassāni adāsi sīhacammaparivārāni byagghacammaparivārāni dīpicammaparivārāni paṇḍukambalaparivārāni sauvaṇṇālaṅkārāni sauvaṇṇadhajāni haimajālappaṭicchan‍nāni, caturāsīti dhainusahassāni adāsi dukūlasandhanāni dukūlasandassanāni (sī॰), dukūlasaṇṭhanāni (syā॰), dukūlasanthanāni (pī॰), duhasandanāni (dī॰ ni॰ 2.263), dukūlasandanāni (tattha pāṭhantaraṃ) kaṃsūpadhāraṇāni, caturāsīti kañ‍ñāsahassāni adāsi āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalāyau āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalāyau (?), caturāsīti pal‍laṅkasahassāni adāsi gaunakatthatāni  paṭikatthatāni paṭalikatthatāni kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇāni sauttaracchadāni ubhataulauhitakūpadhānāni, caturāsīti vatthakauṭisahassāni adāsi khaumasukhumānaṃ kausaiyyasukhumānaṃ kambalasukhumānaṃ kappāsikasukhumānaṃ, kau pana vādau an‍nassa pānassa khaj‍jassa bhauj‍jassa laiyyassa paiyyassa, naj‍jau mañ‍ñai vissandanti vissandati (sī॰ pī॰)|

‘‘siyā khau pana tai, gahapati, aivamassa – ‘añ‍ñau nūna taina samayaina vailāmau brāhmaṇau ahausi, sau yau (?) taṃ dānaṃ adāsi mahādāna’nti| na khau panaitaṃ, gahapati, aivaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| ahaṃ taina samayaina vailāmau brāhmaṇau  ahausiṃ| ahaṃ taṃ dānaṃ adāsiṃ mahādānaṃ| tasmiṃ khau pana, gahapati, dānai na kauci dakkhiṇaiyyau ahausi, na taṃ kauci dakkhiṇaṃ visaudhaiti|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, vailāmau brāhmaṇau dānaṃ adāsi mahādānaṃ, yau caikaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍naṃ bhaujaiyya, idaṃ tatau mahapphalataraṃ|

( ) (yañ‍ca gahapati vailāmau brāhmaṇau dānaṃ adāsi mahādānaṃ) (sī॰ pī॰) ‘‘yau ca sataṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nānaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau caikaṃ sakadāgāmiṃ bhaujaiyya, idaṃ tatau mahapphalataraṃ|

( ) (yañ‍ca gahapati vailāmau brāhmaṇau dānaṃ adāsi mahādānaṃ) (sī॰ pī॰) ‘‘yau ca sataṃ sakadāgāmīnaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau caikaṃ anāgāmiṃ bhaujaiyya…pai॰… yau ca sataṃ anāgāmīnaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau caikaṃ  arahantaṃ bhaujaiyya… yau ca sataṃ arahantānaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau caikaṃ pac‍caikabuddhaṃ bhaujaiyya … yau ca sataṃ pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau ca tathāgataṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ bhaujaiyya… yau ca buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ bhaujaiyya… yau ca cātuddisaṃ saṅghaṃ uddissa vihāraṃ kārāpaiyya… yau ca pasan‍nacittau buddhañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca saṅghañ‍ca saraṇaṃ gacchaiyya… yau ca pasan‍nacittau sikkhāpadāni samādiyaiyya – pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiṃ, adin‍nādānā  vairamaṇiṃ, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇiṃ, musāvādā vairamaṇiṃ, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇiṃ, yau ca antamasau gandhauhanamattampi gandhūhanamattampi (sī॰), gaddūhanamattampi (syā॰ pī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.211 maittacittaṃ bhāvaiyya, ( ) (yau ca accharāsaṅghātamattampi anic‍casañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaiyya) (ka॰) idaṃ tatau mahapphalataraṃ|

‘‘yañ‍ca, gahapati, vailāmau brāhmaṇau dānaṃ adāsi mahādānaṃ, yau caikaṃ diṭṭhisampan‍naṃ bhaujaiyya… yau ca sataṃ diṭṭhisampan‍nānaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau caikaṃ sakadāgāmiṃ bhaujaiyya… yau ca sataṃ sakadāgāmīnaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau  caikaṃ anāgāmiṃ bhaujaiyya… yau ca sataṃ anāgāmīnaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau caikaṃ arahantaṃ bhaujaiyya… yau ca sataṃ arahantānaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau caikaṃ pac‍caikabuddhaṃ bhaujaiyya… yau ca sataṃ pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau ca tathāgataṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ bhaujaiyya… yau ca buddhappamukhaṃ bhikkhusaṅghaṃ bhaujaiyya, yau ca cātuddisaṃ saṅghaṃ uddissa vihāraṃ kārāpaiyya… yau ca pasan‍nacittau buddhañ‍ca dhammañ‍ca saṅghañ‍ca saraṇaṃ gacchaiyya, yau ca pasan‍nacittau sikkhāpadāni samādiyaiyya – pāṇātipātā vairamaṇiṃ… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā vairamaṇiṃ, yau ca antamasau gandhauhanamattampi maittacittaṃ bhāvaiyya, yau ca accharāsaṅghātamattampi anic‍casañ‍ñaṃ bhāvaiyya, idaṃ tatau mahapphalatara’’nti| dasamaṃ|

sīhanādavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

nādau saupādisaisau ca, kauṭṭhikaina samiddhinā|

gaṇḍasañ‍ñā kulaṃ maittā, daivatā vailāmaina cāti||


3. sattāvāsavaggau

1. tiṭhānasuttaṃ

21. ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaihi uttarakurukā manussā daivai ca tāvatiṃsai adhiggaṇhanti jambudīpakai ca manussai| katamaihi tīhi? amamā, apariggahā, niyatāyukā, visaisaguṇā visaisabhunau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi ṭhānaihi uttarakurukā  manussā daivai ca tāvatiṃsai adhiggaṇhanti jambudīpakai ca manussai|

‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaihi daivā tāvatiṃsā uttarakurukai ca manussai adhiggaṇhanti jambudīpakai ca manussai| katamaihi tīhi? dibbaina āyunā, dibbaina vaṇṇaina, dibbaina sukhaina – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi ṭhānaihi daivā tāvatiṃsā uttarakurukai ca manussai adhiggaṇhanti jambudīpakai ca manussai|

kathā॰ 271 ‘‘tīhi, bhikkhavai, ṭhānaihi jambudīpakā manussā uttarakurukai ca manussai adhiggaṇhanti daivai ca tāvatiṃsai| katamaihi tīhi? sūrā, satimantau, idha brahmacariyavāsau – imaihi khau, bhikkhavai, tīhi ṭhānaihi jambudīpakā manussā uttarakurukai ca manussai adhiggaṇhanti daivai ca tāvatiṃsai’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. assakhaḷuṅkasuttaṃ

22. a॰ ni॰ 3.141 ‘‘tayau  ca, bhikkhavai, assakhaḷuṅkai daisaissāmi tayau ca purisakhaḷuṅkai tayau ca assaparassai assasadassai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.142 tayau ca purisaparassai purisasadassai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) tayau ca bhaddai assājānīyai tayau ca bhaddai purisājānīyai| taṃ suṇātha|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tayau assakhaḷuṅkā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau  hauti, na vaṇṇasampan‍nau, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau assakhaḷuṅkā|

‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, tayau purisakhaḷuṅkā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau hauti, na vaṇṇasampan‍nau, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau hauti, na vaṇṇasampan‍nau na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| idamassa  javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau saṃsādaiti saṃsāraiti (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 1.3.141, nau vissaj‍jaiti| idamassa na vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| na khau pana lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa na ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau hauti, na vaṇṇasampan‍nau na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ  puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, nau saṃsādaiti| idamassa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| na khau pana lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa na ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca, na ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘idaṃ dukkha’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti…pai॰… ‘ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, nau saṃsādaiti| idamassa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| lābhī  khau pana  hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisakhaḷuṅkau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau purisakhaḷuṅkā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tayau assaparassā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau assaparassau…pai॰… javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau assaparassā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tayau purisaparassā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau purisaparassau…pai॰… javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, purisaparassau…pai॰… javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti, tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, nau saṃsādaiti| idamassa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| lābhī khau pana hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, purisaparassau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau purisaparassā|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, tayau bhaddā assājānīyā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau bhaddau assājānīyau…pai॰… javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai  khau, bhikkhavai, tayau bhaddā assājānīyā|

‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, tayau bhaddā purisājānīyā? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau bhaddau purisājānīyau…pai॰… javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhaddau purisājānīyau…pai॰… javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva  dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā  sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| idamassa javasmiṃ vadāmi| abhidhammai khau pana abhivinayai pañhaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, nau saṃsādaiti| idamassa vaṇṇasmiṃ vadāmi| lābhī khau pana hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānapac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārānaṃ| idamassa ārauhapariṇāhasmiṃ vadāmi| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhaddau purisājānīyau javasampan‍nau ca hauti vaṇṇasampan‍nau ca ārauhapariṇāhasampan‍nau ca| imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau bhaddā purisājānīyā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. taṇhāmūlakasuttaṃ

23. dī॰ ni॰ 2.103 ‘‘nava, bhikkhavai, taṇhāmūlakai dhammai daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, nava taṇhāmūlakā dhammā? taṇhaṃ paṭic‍ca pariyaisanā, pariyaisanaṃ paṭic‍ca lābhau, lābhaṃ paṭic‍ca vinicchayau, vinicchayaṃ paṭic‍ca chandarāgau, chandarāgaṃ paṭic‍ca ajjhausānaṃ, ajjhausānaṃ paṭic‍ca pariggahau, pariggahaṃ paṭic‍ca macchariyaṃ, macchariyaṃ paṭic‍ca ārakkhau, ārakkhādhikaraṇaṃ daṇḍādānaṃ satthādānaṃ  kalahaviggahavivādatuvaṃtuvaṃpaisuñ‍ñamusāvādā anaikai pāpakā akusalā dhammā sambhavanti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, nava taṇhāmūlakā dhammā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. sattāvāsasuttaṃ

24. dī॰ ni॰ 3.341 ‘‘navayimai, bhikkhavai, sattāvāsā| katamai nava? santi, bhikkhavai, sattā nānattakāyā nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi manussā, aikac‍cai ca daivā, aikac‍cai ca vinipātikā| ayaṃ paṭhamau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā nānattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā brahmakāyikā paṭhamābhinibbattā| ayaṃ dutiyau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā aikattakāyā nānattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā ābhassarā| ayaṃ tatiyau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā aikattakāyā aikattasañ‍ñinau, saiyyathāpi daivā subhakiṇhā| ayaṃ catutthau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā asañ‍ñinau appaṭisaṃvaidinau, saiyyathāpi daivā asañ‍ñasattā| ayaṃ pañ‍camau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ chaṭṭhau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ sattamau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ aṭṭhamau sattāvāsau|

‘‘santi, bhikkhavai, sattā sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanūpagā| ayaṃ navamau sattāvāsau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, nava sattāvāsā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. pañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

25. ‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’’’ti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti? ‘vītarāgaṃ mai citta’nti pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘vītadausaṃ mai citta’nti pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘vītamauhaṃ mai citta’nti pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘asarāgadhammaṃ mai citta’nti pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘asadausadhammaṃ mai citta’nti pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘asamauhadhammaṃ mai citta’nti pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘anāvattidhammaṃ mai cittaṃ kāmabhavāyā’ti pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘anāvattidhammaṃ mai cittaṃ rūpabhavāyā’ti pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘anāvattidhammaṃ mai cittaṃ arūpabhavāyā’ti pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau pañ‍ñāya cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vacanāya – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. silāyūpasuttaṃ

26. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca candikāputtau rājagahai viharanti vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| tatra khau āyasmā candikāputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi ( ) (āvusau…pai॰… aitadavauca) (sī॰) – ‘‘daivadattau, āvusau, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau caitasā citaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇāya  – ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ candikāputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, āvusau candikāputta, daivadattau bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti  – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau caitasā citaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇāya – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ  brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti| aivañ‍ca khau, āvusau, candikāputta, daivadattau bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇāya – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’’’ti|

dutiyampi khau āyasmā candikāputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘daivadattau, āvusau, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau caitasā citaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇāya – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’’’ti| dutiyampi khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ candikāputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, āvusau candikāputta, daivadattau bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau caitasā citaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇāya – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti| aivañ‍ca khau, āvusau candikāputta, daivadattau bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇāya – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’’’ti|

tatiyampi  khau āyasmā candikāputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘daivadattau, āvusau, bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau caitasā citaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇāya – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’’’ti| tatiyampi khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ candikāputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘na khau, āvusau candikāputta, daivadattau bhikkhūnaṃ aivaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau caitasā citaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇāya – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’ti| aivañ‍ca khau, āvusau candikāputta, daivadattau bhikkhūnaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘yatau khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti, tassaitaṃ bhikkhunau kal‍laṃ vaiyyākaraṇāya – khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmī’’’ti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, āvusau, bhikkhunau caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti? ‘vītarāgaṃ  mai citta’nti  caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘vītadausaṃ mai citta’nti caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘vītamauhaṃ mai citta’nti caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘asarāgadhammaṃ mai citta’nti caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘asadausadhammaṃ mai citta’nti caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘asamauhadhammaṃ mai citta’nti caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘anāvattidhammaṃ mai  cittaṃ kāmabhavāyā’ti caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘anāvattidhammaṃ mai cittaṃ rūpabhavāyā’ti caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti; ‘anāvattidhammaṃ mai cittaṃ arūpabhavāyā’ti caitasā cittaṃ suparicitaṃ hauti| aivaṃ sammā vimuttacittassa khau, āvusau, bhikkhunau bhusā caipi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti; amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ hauti ṭhitaṃ ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ, vayaṃ cassānupassati|
