‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, silāyūpau sauḷasakuk‍kukau| tassassu aṭṭha kuk‍kū haiṭṭhā naimaṅgamā, aṭṭha kuk‍kū upari naimassa| atha puratthimāya caipi disāya āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi, naiva naṃ saṅkampaiyya  na sampavaidhaiyya; atha pacchimāya… atha uttarāya… atha dakkhiṇāya caipi disāya  āgacchaiyya bhusā vātavuṭṭhi, naiva naṃ saṅkampaiyya na sampavaidhaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? gambhīrattā, āvusau, naimassa, sunikhātattā silāyūpassa| aivamaivaṃ khau, āvusau, sammā vimuttacittassa bhikkhunau bhusā caipi cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā cakkhussa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti; amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ hauti ṭhitaṃ ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ, vayaṃ cassānupassati|

‘‘bhusā caipi sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā  phauṭṭhabbā… manauviñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā manassa āpāthaṃ āgacchanti, naivassa cittaṃ pariyādiyanti; amissīkatamaivassa cittaṃ hauti ṭhitaṃ ānaiñ‍jappattaṃ, vayaṃ cassānupassatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. paṭhamavairasuttaṃ

27. a॰ ni॰ 9.92; saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1024 atha khau anāthapiṇḍikau gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ  abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau anāthapiṇḍikaṃ gahapatiṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yatau khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, catūhi ca sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau, sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti|

‘‘katamāni  pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti? yaṃ, gahapati, pāṇātipātī pāṇātipātapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau naiva diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, na samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, na caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| pāṇātipātā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti|

‘‘yaṃ, gahapati, adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… kāmaisumicchācārī… musāvādī… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi  dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ  paṭisaṃvaidaiti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau naiva diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, na samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, na caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti| imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, gahapati, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’’’ti|

dhammai  avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘svākkhātau bhagavatā dhammau sandiṭṭhikau akālikau aihipassikau aupanaiyyikau pac‍cattaṃ vaiditabbau viñ‍ñūhī’ti|

saṅghai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘suppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau ujuppaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau ñāyappaṭipan‍nau  bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau sāmīcippaṭipan‍nau bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau; yadidaṃ cattāri purisayugāni aṭṭha purisapuggalā aisa bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghau āhunaiyyau pāhunaiyyau dakkhiṇaiyyau añ‍jalikaraṇīyau anuttaraṃ puñ‍ñakkhaittaṃ laukassā’ti|

ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasatthaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘yatau  khau, gahapati, ariyasāvakassa imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, imaihi ca catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau; sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyavairasuttaṃ

28. saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1025 ‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, catūhi ca sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau; sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti|

‘‘katamāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, pāṇātipātī pāṇātipātapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi  dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, pāṇātipātā paṭiviratau…pai॰… aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, adin‍nādāyī…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhāyī surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānapac‍cayā diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā paṭiviratau naiva diṭṭhadhammikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, na samparāyikampi bhayaṃ vairaṃ pasavati, na caitasikampi dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānā  paṭiviratassa aivaṃ taṃ bhayaṃ vairaṃ vūpasantaṃ hauti| imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti|

‘‘katamaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau buddhai avaic‍cappasādaina saman‍nāgatau hauti – ‘itipi sau bhagavā…pai॰… satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā’ti| dhammai…pai॰… saṅghai… ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti akhaṇḍaihi acchiddaihi asabalaihi akammāsaihi bhujissaihi viñ‍ñuppasatthaihi aparāmaṭṭhaihi samādhisaṃvattanikaihi| imaihi catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti|

‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imāni pañ‍ca bhayāni vairāni vūpasantāni haunti, imaihi ca catūhi sautāpattiyaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau hauti, sau ākaṅkhamānau attanāva attānaṃ byākaraiyya – ‘khīṇanirayaumhi khīṇatiracchānayauni khīṇapaittivisayau khīṇāpāyaduggativinipātau; sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. āghātavatthusuttaṃ

29. vibha॰ 960; dī॰ ni॰ 3.340; a॰ ni॰ 10.79 ‘‘navayimāni, bhikkhavai, āghātavatthūni| katamāni nava? ‘anatthaṃ mai acarī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘anatthaṃ mai caratī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘anatthaṃ mai carissatī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘piyassa mai manāpassa anatthaṃ acarī’ti…pai॰… ‘anatthaṃ caratī’ti…pai॰… ‘anatthaṃ carissatī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati; ‘appiyassa mai  amanāpassa atthaṃ acarī’ti  …pai॰… ‘atthaṃ caratī’ti…pai॰… ‘atthaṃ carissatī’ti āghātaṃ bandhati| imāni khau, bhikkhavai, nava āghātavatthūnī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. āghātapaṭivinayasuttaṃ

30. dī॰ ni॰ 3.340, 359 ‘‘navayimai, bhikkhavai, āghātapaṭivinayā| katamai nava? ‘anatthaṃ mai acari acarīti (syā॰), aivaṃ ‘‘carati, carissati’’ padaisupi, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; ‘anatthaṃ mai carati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; ‘anatthaṃ mai carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; piyassa mai manāpassa anatthaṃ acari…pai॰… anatthaṃ carati…pai॰… ‘anatthaṃ  carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti; appiyassa mai amanāpassa atthaṃ acari…pai॰… atthaṃ carati…pai॰… ‘atthaṃ carissati, taṃ kutaittha labbhā’ti āghātaṃ paṭivinaiti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, nava āghātapaṭivinayā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. anupubbaniraudhasuttaṃ

31. ‘‘navayimai, bhikkhavai, anupubbaniraudhā| katamai nava? paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa kāmasañ‍ñā āmissasañ‍ñā (syā॰) niruddhā hauti; dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa vitak‍kavicārā niruddhā haunti; tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa pīti niruddhā hauti; catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nassa assāsapassāsā niruddhā haunti; ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa rūpasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti; viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti; ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti; naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā niruddhā hauti; sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nassa sañ‍ñā ca vaidanā ca niruddhā haunti| imai khau, bhikkhavai, nava anupubbaniraudhā’’ti dī॰ ni॰ 3.344, 349| aikādasamaṃ|

sattāvāsavaggau tatiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

tiṭhānaṃ khaḷuṅkau taṇhā, sattapañ‍ñā silāyupau|

dvai vairā dvai āghātāni, anupubbaniraudhaina cāti||


4. mahāvaggau

1. anupubbavihārasuttaṃ

32. dī॰ ni॰ 3.344, 359 ‘‘navayimai, bhikkhavai, anupubbavihārā| katamai nava? aittha sī॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu ‘‘idha bhikkhavai bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihī’’ tiādinā vittharaina pāṭhau dissati paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhau – imai khau, bhikkhavai, nava anupubbavihārā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. anupubbavihārasamāpattisuttaṃ

33. ‘‘navayimā, bhikkhavai nava bhikkhavai (?), anupubbavihārasamāpattiyau daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… katamā ca, bhikkhavai, nava anupubbavihārasamāpattiyau? yattha kāmā nirujjhanti, yai ca kāmai niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti, ‘addhā tai āyasmantau nicchātā nibbutā tiṇṇā  pāraṅgatā tadaṅgainā’ti vadāmi| ‘kattha kāmā nirujjhanti, kai ca kāmai niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti – ahamaitaṃ na jānāmi ahamaitaṃ na passāmī’ti, iti yau aivaṃ vadaiyya, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati| aittha kāmā nirujjhanti, tai ca kāmai niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharantī’ti| addhā, bhikkhavai, asaṭhau amāyāvī ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandaiyya anumaudaiyya; ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā namassamānau pañ‍jalikau payirupāsaiyya|

‘‘yattha vitak‍kavicārā nirujjhanti, yai ca vitak‍kavicārai niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti, ‘addhā tai āyasmantau nicchātā nibbutā tiṇṇā pāraṅgatā tadaṅgainā’ti vadāmi| ‘kattha vitak‍kavicārā nirujjhanti, kai ca vitak‍kavicārai niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti – ahamaitaṃ na jānāmi ahamaitaṃ na passāmī’ti, iti yau aivaṃ vadaiyya, sau aivamassa vacanīyau  – ‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; aittha vitak‍kavicārā nirujjhanti, tai ca vitak‍kavicārai niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharantī’ti| addhā, bhikkhavai, asaṭhau amāyāvī ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandaiyya anumaudaiyya; ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā namassamānau pañ‍jalikau payirupāsaiyya|

‘‘yattha pīti nirujjhati, yai ca pītiṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti, ‘addhā tai āyasmantau nicchātā nibbutā tiṇṇā pāraṅgatā tadaṅgainā’ti vadāmi| ‘kattha pīti nirujjhati, kai ca pītiṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti – ahamaitaṃ na jānāmi ahamaitaṃ na passāmī’ti, iti yau aivaṃ vadaiyya, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰…  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; aittha pīti nirujjhati, tai ca pītiṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā  viharantī’ti| addhā, bhikkhavai, asaṭhau amāyāvī ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandaiyya anumaudaiyya; ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā namassamānau pañ‍jalikau payirupāsaiyya|

‘‘yattha upaikkhāsukhaṃ nirujjhati, yai ca upaikkhāsukhaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti, ‘addhā tai āyasmantau nicchātā nibbutā tiṇṇā pāraṅgatā tadaṅgainā’ti vadāmi| ‘kattha upaikkhāsukhaṃ nirujjhati, kai ca upaikkhāsukhaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti – ahamaitaṃ na jānāmi ahamaitaṃ na passāmī’ti, iti yau aivaṃ vadaiyya, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; aittha upaikkhāsukhaṃ nirujjhati, tai ca upaikkhāsukhaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharantī’ti| addhā, bhikkhavai, asaṭhau amāyāvī ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandaiyya  anumaudaiyya; ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā namassamānau pañ‍jalikau payirupāsaiyya|

‘‘yattha rūpasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, yai ca rūpasañ‍ñaṃ yattha rūpasañ‍ñā nirujjhanti, yai ca rūpasañ‍ñā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti, ‘addhā tai āyasmantau nicchātā nibbutā tiṇṇā pāraṅgatā tadaṅgainā’ti vadāmi| ‘kattha rūpasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, kai ca rūpasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti – ahamaitaṃ na jānāmi ahamaitaṃ na passāmī’ti, iti yau aivaṃ vadaiyya, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā anantau ākāsauti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittha rūpasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, tai ca rūpasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharantī’ti| addhā, bhikkhavai, asaṭhau amāyāvī ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandaiyya anumaudaiyya; ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā namassamānau pañ‍jalikau payirupāsaiyya|

‘‘yattha  ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, yai ca ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti, ‘addhā tai āyasmantau nicchātā nibbutā tiṇṇā pāraṅgatā tadaṅgainā’ti vadāmi| ‘kattha ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, kai ca ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti – ahamaitaṃ na jānāmi ahamaitaṃ na passāmī’ti, iti yau aivaṃ vadaiyya, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittha ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, tai ca ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharantī’ti| addhā, bhikkhavai, asaṭhau amāyāvī ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandaiyya anumaudaiyya; ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā namassamānau pañ‍jalikau payirupāsaiyya|

‘‘yattha viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, yai ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti, ‘addhā tai āyasmantau nicchātā nibbutā  tiṇṇā pāraṅgatā tadaṅgainā’ti vadāmi| ‘kattha viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, kai ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti – ahamaitaṃ  na jānāmi ahamaitaṃ na passāmī’ti, iti yau aivaṃ vadaiyya, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma natthi kiñ‍cīti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittha viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, tai ca viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharantī’ti| addhā, bhikkhavai, asaṭhau amāyāvī ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandaiyya anumaudaiyya; ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā namassamānau pañ‍jalikau payirupāsaiyya|

‘‘yattha ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, yai ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti, ‘addhā tai āyasmantau nicchātā nibbutā tiṇṇā pāraṅgatā tadaṅgainā’ti vadāmi| ‘kattha ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, kai ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti – ahamaitaṃ  na jānāmi ahamaitaṃ na passāmī’ti, iti yau aivaṃ vadaiyya, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittha ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, tai ca ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharantī’ti| addhā, bhikkhavai, asaṭhau amāyāvī ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandaiyya anumaudaiyya; ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā namassamānau pañ‍jalikau payirupāsaiyya|

‘‘yattha naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, yai ca naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti, ‘addhā tai  āyasmantau nicchātā nibbutā tiṇṇā pāraṅgatā tadaṅgainā’ti vadāmi| ‘kattha naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, kai ca naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā niraudhaitvā viharanti – ahamaitaṃ na jānāmi ahamaitaṃ na passāmī’ti, iti yau aivaṃ vadaiyya, sau aivamassa vacanīyau – ‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittha naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā nirujjhati, tai ca naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñaṃ niraudhaitvā  niraudhaitvā viharantī’ti| addhā, bhikkhavai, asaṭhau amāyāvī ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinandaiyya anumaudaiyya; ‘sādhū’ti bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā namassamānau pañ‍jalikau payirupāsaiyya| imā khau, bhikkhavai, nava anupubbavihārasamāpattiyau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. nibbānasukhasuttaṃ

34. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| tatra khau āyasmā sāriputtau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘sukhamidaṃ, āvusau, nibbānaṃ| sukhamidaṃ, āvusau, nibbāna’’nti| aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā udāyī āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kiṃ  panaittha, āvusau sāriputta, sukhaṃ yadaittha natthi vaidayita’’nti? ‘‘aitadaiva khvaittha, āvusau, sukhaṃ yadaittha natthi vaidayitaṃ| pañ‍cimai, āvusau, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā  iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā – imai khau, āvusau, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā| yaṃ khau, āvusau, imai pañ‍ca kāmaguṇai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, kāmasukhaṃ|

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau kāmasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svassa hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa tai kāmasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa hauti ābādhau| yau khau panāvusau, ābādhau dukkhamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sukhaṃ nibbānaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau vitak‍kasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā  samudācaranti, svassa hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa tai vitak‍kasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa hauti ābādhau| yau khau panāvusau, ābādhau dukkhamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sukhaṃ nibbānaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau  pītisahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svassa hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa tai pītisahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa hauti ābādhau| yau khau panāvusau, ābādhau dukkhamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sukhaṃ nibbānaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau upaikkhāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svassa hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa tai upaikkhāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa hauti ābādhau| yau khau panāvusau, ābādhau dukkhamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sukhaṃ nibbānaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā anantau ākāsauti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau rūpasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svassa hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa tai  rūpasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa hauti ābādhau| yau khau panāvusau, ābādhau dukkhamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sukhaṃ nibbānaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svassa hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa tai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa hauti ābādhau| yau khau panāvusau, ābādhau dukkhamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sukhaṃ nibbānaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma, natthi kiñ‍cīti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svassa hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa tai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa hauti ābādhau| yau khau panāvusau, ābādhau dukkhamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sukhaṃ nibbānaṃ|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati| tassa cai, āvusau, bhikkhunau iminā vihāraina viharatau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti, svassa hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, āvusau, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa tai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa hauti ābādhau| yau khau panāvusau, ābādhau dukkhamaitaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā| imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sukhaṃ nibbānaṃ|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| imināpi khau aitaṃ, āvusau, pariyāyaina vaiditabbaṃ yathā sukhaṃ nibbāna’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. gāvīupamāsuttaṃ

35. ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gāvī pabbataiyyā bālā abyattā akhaittañ‍ñū akusalā visamai pabbatai carituṃ| tassā aivamassa – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ agatapubbañ‍caiva disaṃ gacchaiyyaṃ, akhāditapubbāni ca tiṇāni khādaiyyaṃ, apītapubbāni ca pānīyāni pivaiyya’nti| sā purimaṃ pādaṃ na suppatiṭṭhitaṃ patiṭṭhāpaitvā pacchimaṃ pādaṃ uddharaiyya| sā na caiva agatapubbaṃ disaṃ gacchaiyya, na ca akhāditapubbāni tiṇāni khādaiyya, na ca apītapubbāni pānīyāni pivaiyya; yasmiṃ cassā padaisai ṭhitāya aivamassa – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ agatapubbañ‍caiva disaṃ gacchaiyyaṃ, akhāditapubbāni  ca tiṇāni khādaiyyaṃ, apītapubbāni  ca pānīyāni pivaiyya’nti tañ‍ca padaisaṃ na sautthinā pac‍cāgacchaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sā, bhikkhavai, gāvī pabbataiyyā bālā abyattā akhaittañ‍ñū akusalā visamai pabbatai carituṃ| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu bālau abyattau akhaittañ‍ñū akusalau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati; sau taṃ nimittaṃ na āsaivati na bhāvaiti na bahulīkarauti na svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharaiyya’nti| sau na sak‍kauti vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| sau na sak‍kauti vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharituṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu ubhatau bhaṭṭhau ubhatau parihīnau, saiyyathāpi sā gāvī pabbataiyyā bālā abyattā akhaittañ‍ñū akusalā visamai pabbatai carituṃ’’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gāvī pabbataiyyā paṇḍitā byattā khaittañ‍ñū kusalā visamai pabbatai carituṃ| tassā aivamassa – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ agatapubbañ‍caiva disaṃ gacchaiyyaṃ, akhāditapubbāni ca tiṇāni khādaiyyaṃ, apītapubbāni  ca pānīyāni pivaiyya’nti| sā purimaṃ pādaṃ suppatiṭṭhitaṃ patiṭṭhāpaitvā pacchimaṃ pādaṃ uddharaiyya| sā agatapubbañ‍caiva disaṃ gacchaiyya, akhāditapubbāni ca tiṇāni  khādaiyya, apītapubbāni ca pānīyāni pivaiyya| yasmiṃ cassā padaisai ṭhitāya aivamassa – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ agatapubbañ‍caiva disaṃ gacchaiyyaṃ, akhāditapubbāni ca tiṇāni khādaiyyaṃ, apītapubbāni ca pānīyāni pivaiyya’nti tañ‍ca padaisaṃ sautthinā pac‍cāgacchaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi sā, bhikkhavai, gāvī pabbataiyyā paṇḍitā byattā khaittañ‍ñū kusalā visamai pabbatai carituṃ| aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, idhaikac‍cau bhikkhu paṇḍitau byattau khaittañ‍ñū kusalau vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ  savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| sau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ anabhihiṃsamānau vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharaiyyaṃ satau ca sampajānau, sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiyyaṃ yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārīti tatiyaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| sau tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ anabhihiṃsamānau pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰…  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| sau catutthaṃ jhānaṃ anabhihiṃsamānau sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā anantau ākāsauti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| sau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ  anabhihiṃsamānau sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā  …pai॰… ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| sau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ anabhihiṃsamānau sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma natthi kiñ‍cīti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| sau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ anabhihiṃsamānau sabbasau  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| sau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ anabhihiṃsamānau sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau taṃ nimittaṃ āsaivati bhāvaiti bahulīkarauti  svādhiṭṭhitaṃ adhiṭṭhāti|

‘‘tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| sau sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ anabhihiṃsamānau sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu taṃ tadaiva samāpattiṃ samāpaj‍jatipi vuṭṭhātipi, tassa mudu cittaṃ hauti kammañ‍ñaṃ| mudunā kammañ‍ñaina cittaina appamāṇau samādhi hauti subhāvitau| sau appamāṇaina samādhinā subhāvitaina yassa yassa abhiñ‍ñāsacchikaraṇīyassa dhammassa cittaṃ  abhinin‍nāmaiti  abhiñ‍ñāsacchikiriyāya tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘anaikavihitaṃ iddhividhaṃ pac‍canubhavaiyyaṃ, aikaupi hutvā bahudhā assaṃ, bahudhāpi hutvā aikau assaṃ…pai॰… yāva brahmalaukāpi kāyaina vasaṃ vattaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – dibbāya  sautadhātuyā…pai॰… sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ caitasā caitau paric‍ca pajānaiyyaṃ, sarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ sarāgaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītarāgaṃ vā cittaṃ vītarāgaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, sadausaṃ vā cittaṃ sadausaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītadausaṃ vā cittaṃ vītadausaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, samauhaṃ vā cittaṃ samauhaṃ cittanti pajānaiyyaṃ, vītamauhaṃ vā cittaṃ… saṃkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… vikkhittaṃ vā cittaṃ… mahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… amahaggataṃ vā cittaṃ… sauttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… anuttaraṃ vā cittaṃ… samāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… asamāhitaṃ vā cittaṃ… vimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ… avimuttaṃ vā cittaṃ avimuttaṃ cittanti pajānaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyyaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – aikampi jātiṃ dvaipi jātiyau…pai॰… iti sākāraṃ sauddaisaṃ anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaraiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau  sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘dibbaina cakkhunā visuddhaina atik‍kantamānusakaina…pai॰… yathākammūpagai sattai pajānaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai|

‘‘sau sacai ākaṅkhati – ‘āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti, tatra tatraiva sakkhibhabbataṃ pāpuṇāti sati sati āyatanai’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. jhānasuttaṃ

36. ‘‘paṭhamampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi; dutiyampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi; tatiyampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi; catutthampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi; ākāsānañ‍cāyatanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi; viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi; ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi; naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi; sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘‘paṭhamampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau  anattatau samanupassati| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaiti patiṭṭhāpaiti (syā॰), paṭipādaiti (ka॰) ma॰ ni॰ 2.133 passitabbaṃ| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaitvā patiṭṭhāpaitvā (syā॰), paṭipādaitvā (ka॰) amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’nti| sau tattha ṭhitau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti| nau cai āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, tainaiva dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, issāsau vā issāsantaivāsī vā tiṇapurisarūpakai vā mattikāpuñ‍jai vā yauggaṃ karitvā, sau aparaina samayaina dūraipātī ca hauti akkhaṇavaidhī ca mahatau ca kāyassa padālaitā padālitā (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.134; 4.181; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassati| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaiti| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaitvā amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’nti| sau tattha ṭhitau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti| nau cai āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, tainaiva dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ‘paṭhamampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘dutiyampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya…pai॰… tatiyampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya… ‘catutthampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau  raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassati| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaiti| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaitvā amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’nti| sau tattha ṭhitau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti| nau cai āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, tainaiva dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, issāsau vā issāsantaivāsī vā tiṇapurisarūpakai vā mattikāpuñ‍jai vā yauggaṃ karitvā, sau aparaina samayaina dūraipātī ca hauti akkhaṇavaidhī ca mahatau ca kāyassa padālaitā; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā, dukkhassa ca pahānā, pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti rūpagataṃ vaidanāgataṃ…pai॰… anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ‘catutthampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘ākāsānañ‍cāyatanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ  viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassati| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaiti| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaitvā amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’nti| sau tattha ṭhitau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti| nau cai āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, tainaiva dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, issāsau vā issāsantaivāsī vā tiṇapurisarūpakai vā mattikāpuñ‍jai vā yauggaṃ karitvā, sau aparaina samayaina dūraipātī ca hauti akkhaṇavaidhī ca mahatau ca kāyassa padālaitā; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ…pai॰… anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ‘ākāsānañ‍cāyatanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘‘viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya…pai॰… ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanampāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī’ti, iti khau panaitaṃ vuttaṃ| kiñ‍caitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma  ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassati| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaiti| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaitvā amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’nti| sau tattha ṭhitau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti| nau cai āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, tainaiva dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, issāsau vā issāsantaivāsī vā tiṇapurisarūpakai vā mattikāpuñ‍jai vā yauggaṃ karitvā, sau aparaina samayaina dūraipātī ca hauti akkhaṇavaidhī ca mahatau ca kāyassa padālaitā; aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau yadaiva tattha hauti vaidanāgataṃ sañ‍ñāgataṃ saṅkhāragataṃ viñ‍ñāṇagataṃ, tai dhammai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau samanupassati| sau taihi dhammaihi  cittaṃ paṭivāpaiti| sau taihi dhammaihi cittaṃ paṭivāpaitvā amatāya dhātuyā cittaṃ upasaṃharati – ‘aitaṃ santaṃ aitaṃ paṇītaṃ yadidaṃ sabbasaṅkhārasamathau sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggau taṇhākkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbāna’nti| sau tattha ṭhitau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti| nau cai āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, tainaiva dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ parikkhayā aupapātikau hauti tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā| ‘ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanampāhaṃ, nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmī’ti, iti yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ, idamaitaṃ paṭic‍ca vuttaṃ|

‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, yāvatā sañ‍ñāsamāpatti tāvatā añ‍ñāpaṭivaidhau| yāni ca khau imāni, bhikkhavai, nissāya dvai āyatanāni – naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasamāpatti ca sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhau  ca, jhāyīhaitai, bhikkhavai, samāpattikusalaihi samāpattivuṭṭhānakusalaihi samāpaj‍jitvā vuṭṭhahitvā sammā akkhātabbānīti vadāmī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. ānandasuttaṃ

37. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| tatra khau āyasmā ānandau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘acchariyaṃ, āvusau, abbhutaṃ, āvusau! yāvañ‍cidaṃ taina bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sambādhai aukāsādhigamau  anubuddhau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā saukaparidaivānaṃ samatik‍kamāya dukkhadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamāya ñāyassa adhigamāya nibbānassa sacchikiriyāya| tadaiva nāma cakkhuṃ bhavissati tai rūpā tañ‍cāyatanaṃ  nau paṭisaṃvaidissati paṭisaṃvaidayati (ka॰)| tadaiva nāma sautaṃ bhavissati tai saddā tañ‍cāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidissati| tadaiva nāma ghānaṃ bhavissati tai gandhā tañ‍cāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidissati| sāva nāma jivhā bhavissati tai rasā tañ‍cāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidissati| sauva nāma kāyau bhavissati tai phauṭṭhabbā tañ‍cāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidissatī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai āyasmā udāyī āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sañ‍ñīmaiva nu khau, āvusau ānanda, tadāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidaiti udāhu asañ‍ñī’’ti? ‘‘sañ‍ñīmaiva khau, āvusau, tadāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidaiti, nau asañ‍ñī’’ti|

‘‘kiṃsañ‍ñī panāvusau, tadāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidaitī’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu, sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃsañ‍ñīpi khau, āvusau, tadāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃsañ‍ñīpi khau, āvusau, tadāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidaiti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aivaṃsañ‍ñīpi khau, āvusau, tadāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidaitī’’ti|

‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, āvusau, samayaṃ sākaitai viharāmi añ‍janavanai migadāyai| atha khau, āvusau, jaṭilavāsikā jaṭilagāhiyā (sī॰ pī॰), jaḍilabhāgikā (syā॰) bhikkhunī  yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā maṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhāsi| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, āvusau, jaṭilavāsikā bhikkhunī maṃ aitadavauca – ‘yāyaṃ, bhantai ānanda, samādhi na cābhinatau na cāpanatau na ca sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritagatau sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritavatau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), sasaṅkhāraniggayhavārivāvaṭau (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 3.102; 5.27; dī॰ ni॰ 3.355, vimuttattā ṭhitau, ṭhitattā santusitau, santusitattā nau paritassati| ayaṃ, bhantai ānanda, samādhi kiṃphalau vuttau bhagavatā’’’ti?

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, sauhaṃ, āvusau, jaṭilavāsikaṃ bhikkhuniṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘yāyaṃ, bhagini, samādhi na cābhinatau na cāpanatau na ca sasaṅkhāraniggayhavāritagatau, vimuttattā ṭhitau, ṭhitattā santusitau, santusitattā nau paritassati| ayaṃ, bhagini, samādhi añ‍ñāphalau vuttau bhagavatā’ti| aivaṃsañ‍ñīpi khau, āvusau, tadāyatanaṃ nau paṭisaṃvaidaitī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. laukāyatikasuttaṃ

38. atha khau dvai laukāyatikā brāhmaṇā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai brāhmaṇā bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘pūraṇau, bhau gautama, kassapau sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘caratau ca mai tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa  ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ  pac‍cupaṭṭhita’nti| sau aivamāha – ‘ahaṃ anantaina ñāṇaina anantaṃ laukaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ  viharāmī’ti| ayampi  ayampi hi (syā॰ ka॰), bhau gautama, nigaṇṭhau nāṭaputtau sabbañ‍ñū sabbadassāvī aparisaisaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ paṭijānāti – ‘caratau ca mai tiṭṭhatau ca suttassa ca jāgarassa ca satataṃ samitaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ pac‍cupaṭṭhita’nti| sau saupi (?) aivamāha – ‘ahaṃ anantaina ñāṇaina anantaṃ laukaṃ jānaṃ passaṃ viharāmī’ti| imaisaṃ, bhau gautama, ubhin‍naṃ ñāṇavādānaṃ ubhin‍naṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vipac‍canīkavādānaṃ kau sac‍caṃ āha kau musā’’ti?

‘‘alaṃ, brāhmaṇā! tiṭṭhataitaṃ – ‘imaisaṃ ubhin‍naṃ ñāṇavādānaṃ ubhin‍naṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ vipac‍canīkavādānaṃ kau sac‍caṃ āha kau musā’ti| dhammaṃ vau, brāhmaṇā, daisaissāmi, taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhau’’ti khau tai brāhmaṇā bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘saiyyathāpi, brāhmaṇā, cattārau purisā catuddisā ṭhitā paramaina javaina ca saman‍nāgatā paramaina ca padavītihāraina| tai aivarūpaina javaina saman‍nāgatā assu, saiyyathāpi nāma daḷhadhammā daḷhadhammau (sabbattha) a॰ ni॰ 4.45; ma॰ ni॰ 1.116 ca, taṃsaṃvaṇṇanāṭīkāyau ca mauggal‍lānabyākaraṇañ‍ca aulaukaitabbā dhanuggahau sikkhitau katahatthau katūpāsanau lahukaina asanaina appakasiraina tiriyaṃ tālacchāyaṃ tālacchātiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), tālacchādiṃ (ka॰) a॰ ni॰ 4.45; ma॰ ni॰ 1.161 passitabbaṃ atipātaiyya; aivarūpaina ca padavītihāraina, saiyyathāpi nāma puratthimā samuddā pacchimau samuddau atha puratthimāya disāya ṭhitau purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ pāpuṇissāmī’ti| sau añ‍ñatraiva asitapītakhāyitasāyitā añ‍ñatra uc‍cārapassāvakammā añ‍ñatra niddākilamathapaṭivinaudanā  vassasatāyukau vassasatajīvī vassasataṃ gantvā appatvāva laukassa antaṃ antarā kālaṃ  karaiyya| atha pacchimāya disāya…pai॰… atha uttarāya disāya… atha dakkhiṇāya disāya ṭhitau purisau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahaṃ gamanaina laukassa antaṃ pāpuṇissāmī’ti| sau añ‍ñatraiva asitapītakhāyitasāyitā añ‍ñatra uc‍cārapassāvakammā añ‍ñatra  niddākilamathapaṭivinaudanā vassasatāyukau vassasatajīvī vassasataṃ gantvā appatvāva laukassa antaṃ antarā kālaṃ karaiyya| taṃ kissa haitu? nāhaṃ, brāhmaṇā, aivarūpāya sandhāvanikāya laukassa antaṃ ñātaiyyaṃ daṭṭhaiyyaṃ pattaiyyanti vadāmi| na cāhaṃ, brāhmaṇā, appatvāva laukassa antaṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmi|

‘‘pañ‍cimai, brāhmaṇā, kāmaguṇā ariyassa vinayai laukauti vuc‍cati| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā  rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā; sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā; imai khau, brāhmaṇā, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā ariyassa vinayai laukauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘idha, brāhmaṇā, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇā, ‘bhikkhu laukassa antamāgamma, laukassa antai viharati’| tamañ‍ñai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayampi  laukapariyāpan‍nau, ayampi anissaṭau laukamhā’ti| ahampi hi ahampi (sī॰ pī॰), brāhmaṇā, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ayampi laukapariyāpan‍nau, ayampi anissaṭau laukamhā’’’ti|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, brāhmaṇā, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇā, ‘bhikkhu laukassa antamāgamma laukassa antai viharati’| tamañ‍ñai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayampi laukapariyāpan‍nau, ayampi anissaṭau laukamhā’ti| ahampi hi, brāhmaṇā, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ayampi laukapariyāpan‍nau, ayampi anissaṭau laukamhā’’’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇā, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇā, ‘bhikkhu laukassa antamāgamma laukassa antai viharati’| tamañ‍ñai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayampi laukapariyāpan‍nau, ayampi anissaṭau laukamhā’ti| ahampi hi, brāhmaṇā, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ayampi laukapariyāpan‍nau, ayampi anissaṭau laukamhā’’’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇā, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati…pai॰… sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇā, ‘bhikkhu laukassa antamāgamma laukassa antai viharati’| tamañ‍ñai aivamāhaṃsu – ‘ayampi laukapariyāpan‍nau, ayampi anissaṭau laukamhā’ti| ahampi hi, brāhmaṇā, aivaṃ vadāmi – ‘ayampi laukapariyāpan‍nau, ayampi anissaṭau laukamhā’’’ti|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, brāhmaṇā, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, brāhmaṇā, ‘bhikkhu laukassa  antamāgamma laukassa antai viharati tiṇṇau laukai visattika’’’nti| sattamaṃ|

8. daivāsurasaṅgāmasuttaṃ

39. ‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivāsurasaṅgāmau samupabyūḷhau samupabbūḷhau (sī॰ pī॰) ahausi| tasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, saṅgāmai asurā jiniṃsu, daivā parājayiṃsu parājiyiṃsu (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰)| parājitā ca, bhikkhavai, daivā daivā bhītā (pī॰) apayiṃsuyaiva apayaṃsvaiva (sī॰) uttarainābhimukhā, abhiyiṃsu abhiyaṃsu (sī॰) asurā| atha khau, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘abhiyantaiva khau asurā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ dutiyampi asuraihi saṅgāmaiyyāmā’ti| dutiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, daivā asuraihi saṅgāmaisuṃ| dutiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, asurāva jiniṃsu, daivā parājayiṃsu| parājitā ca, bhikkhavai, daivā apayiṃsuyaiva uttarainābhimukhā, abhiyiṃsu asurā’’|

atha khau, bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘abhiyantaiva khau asurā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ tatiyampi asuraihi saṅgāmaiyyāmā’ti| tatiyampi khau, bhikkhavai,  daivā asuraihi saṅgāmaisuṃ| tatiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, asurāva jiniṃsu, daivā parājayiṃsu| parājitā ca, bhikkhavai, daivā bhītā daivapuraṃyaiva pavisiṃsu| daivapuragatānañ‍ca pana puna (ka॰), bhikkhavai, daivānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘bhīruttānagataina khau dāni mayaṃ aitarahi  attanā viharāma akaraṇīyā asuraihī’ti| asurānampi, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘bhīruttānagataina khau dāni daivā aitarahi attanā viharanti akaraṇīyā amhaihī’ti|

‘‘bhūtapubbaṃ, bhikkhavai, daivāsurasaṅgāmau samupabyūḷhau ahausi| tasmiṃ khau pana, bhikkhavai, saṅgāmai daivā jiniṃsu, asurā parājayiṃsu| parājitā ca, bhikkhavai, asurā apayiṃsuyaiva dakkhiṇainābhimukhā, abhiyiṃsu daivā| atha khau, bhikkhavai, asurānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘abhiyantaiva khau daivā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ dutiyampi daivaihi saṅgāmaiyyāmā’ti| dutiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, asurā daivaihi saṅgāmaisuṃ| dutiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, daivā jiniṃsu, asurā parājayiṃsu| parājitā ca, bhikkhavai, asurā apayiṃsuyaiva dakkhiṇainābhimukhā, abhiyiṃsu daivā’’|

atha khau, bhikkhavai, asurānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘abhiyantaiva khau daivā| yaṃnūna mayaṃ tatiyampi daivaihi saṅgāmaiyyāmā’ti| tatiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, asurā daivaihi saṅgāmaisuṃ| tatiyampi khau, bhikkhavai, daivā jiniṃsu, asurā parājayiṃsu| parājitā ca, bhikkhavai, asurā bhītā asurapuraṃyaiva pavisiṃsu| asurapuragatānañ‍ca pana, bhikkhavai, asurānaṃ aitadahausi – ‘bhīruttānagataina khau dāni mayaṃ aitarahi attanā viharāma akaraṇīyā  daivaihī’ti| daivānampi, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘bhīruttānagataina khau dāni asurā aitarahi attanā viharanti akaraṇīyā amhaihī’ti|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhikkhussa aivaṃ hauti – ‘bhīruttānagataina khau dānāhaṃ aitarahi attanā viharāmi  akaraṇīyau mārassā’ti| mārassāpi, bhikkhavai, pāpimatau aivaṃ hauti – ‘bhīruttānagataina khau dāni bhikkhu aitarahi attanā viharati akaraṇīyau mayha’’’nti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, tasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai  bhikkhussa aivaṃ hauti – ‘bhīruttānagataina khau dānāhaṃ aitarahi attanā viharāmi akaraṇīyau mārassā’ti| mārassāpi, bhikkhavai, pāpimatau aivaṃ hauti – ‘bhīruttānagataina khau dāni bhikkhu aitarahi attanā viharati, akaraṇīyau mayha’’’nti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu antamakāsi māraṃ, apadaṃ vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau tiṇṇau laukai visattika’’’nti|

‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati… sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi  kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati… sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati… sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘bhikkhu antamakāsi māraṃ, apadaṃ vadhitvā māracakkhuṃ adassanaṃ gatau pāpimatau tiṇṇau laukai visattika’’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. nāgasuttaṃ

40. ‘‘yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ārañ‍ñikassa nāgassa gaucarapasutassa hatthīpi hatthiniyaupi hatthikalabhāpi hatthicchāpāpi puratau puratau gantvā tiṇaggāni chindanti, taina, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikau nāgau aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ārañ‍ñikassa nāgassa gaucarapasutassa hatthīpi hatthiniyaupi hatthikalabhāpi hatthicchāpāpi aubhaggaubhaggaṃ sākhābhaṅgaṃ khādanti, taina, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikau nāgau aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ārañ‍ñikassa nāgassa augāhaṃ autiṇṇassa hatthīpi hatthiniyaupi hatthikalabhāpi hatthicchāpāpi puratau puratau gantvā sauṇḍāya udakaṃ ālauḷainti, taina, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikau nāgau aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati| yasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ārañ‍ñikassa  nāgassa augāhā uttiṇṇassa hatthiniyau kāyaṃ upanighaṃsantiyau gacchanti, taina, bhikkhavai, ārañ‍ñikau nāgau aṭṭīyati harāyati jigucchati|

‘‘tasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ārañ‍ñikassa nāgassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi ākiṇṇau viharāmi hatthīhi hatthinīhi hatthikalabhaihi hatthicchāpaihi| chin‍naggāni caiva tiṇāni khādāmi, aubhaggaubhaggañ‍ca mai sākhābhaṅgaṃ khādanti khāditaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) mahāva॰ 467 passitabbaṃ, āvilāni ca pānīyāni pivāmi, augāhā ca augāhāpi ca (syā॰ ka॰) mahāva॰ 467 passitabbaṃ mai uttiṇṇassa hatthiniyau kāyaṃ upanighaṃsantiyau gacchanti| yaṃnūnāhaṃ aikau gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭhau viharaiyya’nti| sau aparaina samayaina aikau gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭhau viharati, acchin‍naggāni caiva tiṇāni khādati, aubhaggaubhaggañ‍cassa sākhābhaṅgaṃ  na khādanti na aubhaggaubhaggañ‍ca sākhābhaṅga khādati (syā॰ ka॰), anāvilāni ca pānīyāni pivati, augāhā cassa uttiṇṇassa na hatthiniyau kāyaṃ upanighaṃsantiyau gacchanti|

‘‘tasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai ārañ‍ñikassa nāgassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau pubbai ākiṇṇau vihāsiṃ hatthīhi hatthinīhi hatthikalabhaihi hatthicchāpaihi, chin‍naggāni caiva tiṇāni khādiṃ, aubhaggaubhaggañ‍ca mai sākhābhaṅgaṃ khādiṃsu, āvilāni ca pānīyāni apāyiṃ, augāhā aittha pisaddau sabbatthapi natthi ca mai uttiṇṇassa hatthiniyau kāyaṃ upanighaṃsantiyau agamaṃsu| sauhaṃ aitarahi aikau gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭhau viharāmi, acchin‍naggāni caiva tiṇāni khādāmi, aubhaggaubhaggañ‍ca mai sākhābhaṅgaṃ na khādanti, anāvilāni ca pānīyāni pivāmi, augāhā ca mai uttiṇṇassa na hatthiniyau kāyaṃ upanighaṃsantiyau gacchantī’ti| sau sauṇḍāya sākhābhaṅgaṃ bhañ‍jitvā sākhābhaṅgaina kāyaṃ parimaj‍jitvā attamanau sauṇḍaṃ saṃharati kaṇḍuṃ saṃhanti (sī॰ pī॰) kaṇḍuṃ saṃhanati (syā॰), aittha kaṇḍuvanadukkhaṃ vinaitīti atthau,|

‘‘aivamaivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, yasmiṃ samayai bhikkhu ākiṇṇau viharati bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakaihi upāsikāhi rañ‍ñā rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi  titthiyasāvakaihi, tasmiṃ, bhikkhavai, samayai bhikkhussa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi ākiṇṇau viharāmi bhikkhūhi bhikkhunīhi upāsakaihi  upāsikāhi rañ‍ñā rājamahāmattaihi titthiyaihi titthiyasāvakaihi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ aikau gaṇasmā vūpakaṭṭhau viharaiyya’nti| sau vivittaṃ saināsanaṃ bhajati arañ‍ñaṃ rukkhamūlaṃ  pabbataṃ kandaraṃ giriguhaṃ susānaṃ vanapatthaṃ abbhaukāsaṃ palālapuñ‍jaṃ| sau arañ‍ñagatau vā rukkhamūlagatau vā suñ‍ñāgāragatau vā nisīdati pal‍laṅkaṃ ābhujitvā ujuṃ kāyaṃ paṇidhāya parimukhaṃ satiṃ upaṭṭhapaitvā|

‘‘sau abhijjhaṃ laukai pahāya vigatābhijjhaina caitasā viharati, abhijjhāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; byāpādapadausaṃ pahāya abyāpan‍nacittau viharati sabbapāṇabhūtahitānukampī, byāpādapadausā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; thinamiddhaṃ pahāya vigatathinamiddhau viharati ālaukasañ‍ñī satau sampajānau, thinamiddhā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ pahāya anuddhatau viharati ajjhattaṃ vūpasantacittau, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cā cittaṃ parisaudhaiti; vicikicchaṃ pahāya tiṇṇavicikicchau viharati akathaṃkathī kusalaisu dhammaisu, vicikicchāya cittaṃ parisaudhaiti| sau imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇai pahāya caitasau upak‍kilaisai pañ‍ñāya dubbalīkaraṇai vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau attamanau sauṇḍaṃ saṃharati kaṇḍuṃ saṃhanti (sī॰ pī॰), kaṇḍuṃ saṃhanati (syā॰), aittha kaṇḍuvanasadisaṃ jhānapaṭipakkhaṃ kilaisadukkhaṃ vinaitīti atthau| vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau attamanau sauṇḍaṃ saṃharati|

‘‘sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| sau attamanau sauṇḍaṃ  saṃharati| sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati… sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati… sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati… sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ  samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| sau attamanau sauṇḍaṃ saṃharatī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tapussasuttaṃ

41. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā mal‍laisu viharati uruvailakappaṃ nāma mal‍lānaṃ nigamau| atha khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya uruvailakappaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| uruvailakappai piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘idhaiva tāva tvaṃ, ānanda, hauhi, yāvāhaṃ mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhāmi divāvihārāyā’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| atha khau bhagavā mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi|

atha khau tapussau gahapati yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau tapussau gahapati āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘mayaṃ, bhantai ānanda, gihī kāmabhauginau kāmārāmā kāmaratā kāmasammuditā| taisaṃ nau, bhantai, amhākaṃ gihīnaṃ kāmabhaugīnaṃ kāmārāmānaṃ kāmaratānaṃ kāmasammuditānaṃ  papātau viya khāyati, yadidaṃ naikkhammaṃ| sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, ‘imasmiṃ dhammavinayai daharānaṃ daharānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ naikkhammai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’ passataṃ (?)| tayidaṃ, bhantai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai bhikkhūnaṃ bahunā janaina visabhāgau, yadidaṃ naikkhamma’’nti|

‘‘atthi  khau aitaṃ, gahapati, kathāpābhataṃ bhagavantaṃ dassanāya| āyāma, gahapati, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamissāma; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaissāma| yathā nau bhagavā byākarissati tathā naṃ dhāraissāmā’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tapussau gahapati āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausi| atha khau āyasmā ānandau tapussaina gahapatinā saddhiṃ  yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘ayaṃ, bhantai, tapussau gahapati aivamāha – ‘mayaṃ, bhantai ānanda, gihī kāmabhauginau kāmārāmā kāmaratā kāmasammuditā, taisaṃ nau bhantai, amhākaṃ gihīnaṃ kāmabhaugīnaṃ kāmārāmānaṃ kāmaratānaṃ kāmasammuditānaṃ papātau viya khāyati, yadidaṃ naikkhammaṃ’| sutaṃ maitaṃ, bhantai, ‘imasmiṃ dhammavinayai daharānaṃ daharānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ naikkhammai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| tayidaṃ, bhantai, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai bhikkhūnaṃ bahunā janaina visabhāgau yadidaṃ naikkhamma’’’nti|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ, ānanda, aivamaitaṃ, ānanda! mayhampi khau, ānanda, pubbaiva sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva satau aitadahausi – ‘sādhu naikkhammaṃ, sādhu pavivaikau’ti| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, naikkhammai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina mai naikkhammai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’? tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘kāmaisu khau mai ādīnavau adiṭṭhau, sau ca mai abahulīkatau, naikkhammai ca ānisaṃsau anadhigatau, sau ca mai anāsaivitau| tasmā mai naikkhammai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ kāmaisu ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulaṃ karaiyyaṃ bahulīkaraiyyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaivaiyyaṃ, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai naikkhammai cittaṃ pakkhandaiyya pasīdaiyya santiṭṭhaiyya vimuc‍caiyya aitaṃ santanti passatau’| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, aparaina samayaina kāmaisu ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulamakāsiṃ, naikkhammai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaiviṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, naikkhammai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, iminā vihāraina viharatau kāmasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya  yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa mai kāmasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, avitak‍kai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina mai avitak‍kai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’? tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘vitak‍kaisu khau mai ādīnavau adiṭṭhau, sau ca mai abahulīkatau, avitak‍kai ca ānisaṃsau anadhigatau, sau ca  mai anāsaivitau| tasmā mai avitak‍kai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ vitak‍kaisu ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulaṃ karaiyyaṃ, avitak‍kai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaivaiyyaṃ, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai avitak‍kai cittaṃ pakkhandaiyya pasīdaiyya santiṭṭhaiyya vimuc‍caiyya aitaṃ santanti passatau’| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, aparaina samayaina vitak‍kaisu ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulamakāsiṃ, avitak‍kai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaiviṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, avitak‍kai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, iminā vihāraina viharatau vitak‍kasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa mai vitak‍kasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca viharaiyyaṃ satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiyyaṃ yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti – upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārīti tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, nippītikai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina mai nippītikai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’? tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘pītiyā khau mai ādīnavau adiṭṭhau, sau ca mai abahulīkatau, nippītikai ca ānisaṃsau anadhigatau, sau ca mai anāsaivitau| tasmā mai nippītikai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati  na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ pītiyā ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ  bahulaṃ karaiyyaṃ, nippītikai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaivaiyyaṃ, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai nippītikai cittaṃ pakkhandaiyya pasīdaiyya santiṭṭhaiyya vimuc‍caiyya aitaṃ santanti passatau’| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, aparaina samayaina pītiyā ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulamakāsiṃ, nippītikai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaiviṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, nippītikai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| sau  khau ahaṃ, ānanda, pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, iminā vihāraina viharatau pītisahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa mai pītisahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, adukkhamasukhai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina mai adukkhamasukhai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’? tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘upaikkhāsukhai khau mai ādīnavau adiṭṭhau, sau ca mai abahulīkatau, adukkhamasukhai ca ānisaṃsau anadhigatau, sau ca mai anāsaivitau| tasmā mai adukkhamasukhai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ  santanti passatau’| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ upaikkhāsukhai ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ  bahulaṃ karaiyyaṃ, adukkhamasukhai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaivaiyyaṃ, ṭhānaṃ  khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai adukkhamasukhai cittaṃ pakkhandaiyya pasīdaiyya santiṭṭhaiyya vimuc‍caiyya aitaṃ santanti passatau’| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, aparaina samayaina upaikkhāsukhai ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulamakāsiṃ adukkhamasukhai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaiviṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, adukkhamasukhai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰…  catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, iminā vihāraina viharatau upaikkhāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa mai upaikkhāsahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘‘anantau ākāsau’’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina  mai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’? tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘rūpaisu khau mai ādīnavau adiṭṭhau, sau ca abahulīkatau, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ca ānisaṃsau anadhigatau, sau ca mai anāsaivitau| tasmā mai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti  passatau’| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ rūpaisu ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulaṃ karaiyyaṃ, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaivaiyyaṃ, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ pakkhandaiyya pasīdaiyya santiṭṭhaiyya vimuc‍caiyya aitaṃ santanti passatau’| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, aparaina samayaina rūpaisu ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulamakāsiṃ, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaiviṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā  paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, iminā vihāraina viharatau rūpasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa mai rūpasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau|

‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma  ‘‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina mai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’? tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai khau mai ādīnavau adiṭṭhau, sau ca abahulīkatau, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai ca ānisaṃsau anadhigatau, sau ca mai anāsaivitau| tasmā mai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulaṃ  karaiyyaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaivaiyyaṃ, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ pakkhandaiyya pasīdaiyya santiṭṭhaiyya vimuc‍caiyya aitaṃ santanti passatau’| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, aparaina samayaina ākāsānañ‍cāyatanai ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulamakāsiṃ, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaiviṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, iminā vihāraina viharatau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa mai ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma natthi kiñ‍cīti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina mai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’? tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai khau mai ādīnavau adiṭṭhau, sau ca mai abahulīkatau, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai ca ānisaṃsau anadhigatau, sau ca mai anāsaivitau| tasmā mai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati  na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulaṃ karaiyyaṃ, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaivaiyyaṃ, ṭhānaṃ khau  panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai  cittaṃ pakkhandaiyya pasīdaiyya santiṭṭhaiyya vimuc‍caiyya aitaṃ santanti passatau’| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, aparaina samayaina viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanai ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulamakāsiṃ, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaiviṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, iminā vihāraina viharatau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa mai viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi  – ‘kau nu khau haitu kau pac‍cayau, yaina mai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’? tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai khau mai ādīnavau adiṭṭhau, sau ca mai abahulīkatau, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai  ca ānisaṃsau anadhigatau, sau ca mai anāsaivitau| tasmā mai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulaṃ karaiyyaṃ, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaivaiyyaṃ, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ pakkhandaiyya pasīdaiyya santiṭṭhaiyya vimuc‍caiyya aitaṃ santanti passatau’| sau  khau ahaṃ, ānanda, aparaina samayaina ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanai ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulamakāsiṃ, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaiviṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti  passatau| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, iminā vihāraina viharatau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau| saiyyathāpi, ānanda, sukhinau dukkhaṃ uppaj‍jaiyya yāvadaiva ābādhāya; aivamaivassa mai ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| svassa mai hauti ābādhau|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharaiyya’nti| tassa  mayhaṃ, ānanda, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina mai sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’? tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai khau mai ādīnavau adiṭṭhau, sau ca mai abahulīkatau, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhai ca ānisaṃsau anadhigatau, sau  ca mai anāsaivitau| tasmā mai sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati na vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau’| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau ahaṃ naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulaṃ karaiyyaṃ, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaivaiyyaṃ, ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ vij‍jati yaṃ mai sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhai cittaṃ pakkhandaiyya pasīdaiyya santiṭṭhaiyya vimuc‍caiyya aitaṃ santanti passatau’| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, aparaina samayaina naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanai ādīnavaṃ disvā taṃ bahulamakāsiṃ, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhai ānisaṃsaṃ adhigamma tamāsaiviṃ| tassa mayhaṃ, ānanda, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhai cittaṃ pakkhandati pasīdati santiṭṭhati vimuc‍cati aitaṃ santanti passatau| sau khau ahaṃ, ānanda, sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi, pañ‍ñāya ca mai disvā āsavā parikkhayaṃ agamaṃsu|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, ānanda, imā nava anupubbavihārasamāpattiyau na aivaṃ anulaumapaṭilaumaṃ samāpaj‍jimpi vuṭṭhahimpi, naiva tāvāhaṃ, ānanda, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| yatau ca khau ahaṃ, ānanda, imā nava anupubbavihārasamāpattiyau aivaṃ anulaumapaṭilaumaṃ  samāpaj‍jimpi vuṭṭhahimpi, athāhaṃ, ānanda, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya ‘anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhau’ti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai caitauvimutti vimutti (ka॰ sī॰ ka॰), ayamantimā jāti, natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| dasamaṃ|

mahāvaggau catutthau|


tassuddānaṃ –

dvai vihārā ca nibbānaṃ, gāvī jhānaina pañ‍camaṃ|

ānandau brāhmaṇā daivau, nāgaina tapussaina cāti||


5. sāmañ‍ñavaggau

1. sambādhasuttaṃ

42. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha khau āyasmā udāyī yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau  khau āyasmā udāyī āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vuttamidaṃ, āvusau, pañ‍cālacaṇḍaina daivaputtaina –

‘‘sambādhai gataṃ sambādhai vata (sī॰) aukāsaṃ, avidvā bhūrimaidhasau|

yau jhānamabujjhi buddhau, paṭilīnanisabhau munī’’ti||

‘‘katamau, āvusau, sambādhau, katamau sambādhai aukāsādhigamau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, kāmaguṇā sambādhau vuttau bhagavatā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, āvusau, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā sambādhau vuttau bhagavatā|

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sambādhai aukāsādhigamau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina| tatrāpatthi sambādhau| kiñ‍ca tattha sambādhau? yadaiva  tattha vitak‍kavicārā aniruddhā haunti, ayamaittha sambādhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sambādhai aukāsādhigamau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina| tatrāpatthi sambādhau| kiñ‍ca tattha sambādhau? yadaiva tattha pīti aniruddhā hauti, ayamaittha sambādhau|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu pītiyā ca virāgā…pai॰… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sambādhai aukāsādhigamau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina| tatrāpatthi sambādhau| kiñ‍ca tattha sambādhau? yadaiva tattha upaikkhāsukhaṃ aniruddhaṃ hauti, ayamaittha sambādhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sukhassa ca pahānā…pai॰… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sambādhai aukāsādhigamau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina| tatrāpatthi sambādhau| kiñ‍ca tattha sambādhau? yadaiva tattha rūpasañ‍ñā aniruddhā hauti, ayamaittha sambādhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sambādhai aukāsādhigamau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina| tatrāpatthi sambādhau| kiñ‍ca tattha sambādhau? yadaiva tattha ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā aniruddhā hauti, ayamaittha sambādhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sambādhai aukāsādhigamau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina| tatrāpatthi sambādhau| kiñ‍ca tattha sambādhau? yadaiva tattha viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā aniruddhā hauti, ayamaittha sambādhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma ‘natthi kiñ‍cī’ti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sambādhai aukāsādhigamau  vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina| tatrāpatthi sambādhau| kiñ‍ca tattha sambādhau? yadaiva tattha ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā aniruddhā hauti, ayamaittha sambādhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sambādhai aukāsādhigamau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina| tatrāpatthi  sambādhau| kiñ‍ca tattha sambādhau? yadaiva tattha naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasañ‍ñā aniruddhā hauti, ayamaittha sambādhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sambādhai aukāsādhigamau vuttau bhagavatā nippariyāyainā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. kāyasakkhīsuttaṃ

43. ‘‘‘kāyasakkhī kāyasakkhī’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, kāyasakkhī vuttau bhagavatā’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yathā yathā ca tadāyatanaṃ tathā tathā naṃ kāyaina phusitvā viharati| aittāvatāpi  khau, āvusau, kāyasakkhī vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā…pai॰… dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ… tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ… catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yathā yathā ca tadāyatanaṃ tathā tathā naṃ kāyaina phusitvā viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, kāyasakkhī vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā  nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā ‘anantau ākāsau’ti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yathā yathā ca tadāyatanaṃ tathā tathā naṃ kāyaina phusitvā viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, kāyasakkhī vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| yathā yathā ca tadāyatanaṃ tathā tathā naṃ kāyaina phusitvā viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, kāyasakkhī vuttau bhagavatā nippariyāyainā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. pañ‍ñāvimuttasuttaṃ

44. ‘‘‘pañ‍ñāvimuttau  pañ‍ñāvimuttau’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, pañ‍ñāvimuttau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya ca naṃ pajānāti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, pañ‍ñāvimuttau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti, pañ‍ñāya ca naṃ pajānāti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, pañ‍ñāvimuttau vuttau bhagavatā nippariyāyainā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. ubhataubhāgavimuttasuttaṃ

45. ‘‘‘ubhataubhāgavimuttau  ubhataubhāgavimuttau’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, ubhataubhāgavimuttau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti?
