‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| yathā yathā ca tadāyatanaṃ tathā tathā naṃ kāyaina phusitvā viharati, pañ‍ñāya ca naṃ pajānāti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ubhataubhāgavimuttau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina…pai॰…|

‘‘puna  caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| yathā yathā ca tadāyatanaṃ tathā tathā naṃ kāyaina phusitvā viharati, pañ‍ñāya ca naṃ pajānāti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, ubhataubhāgavimuttau vuttau bhagavatā nippariyāyainā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. sandiṭṭhikadhammasuttaṃ

46. ‘‘‘sandiṭṭhikau dhammau sandiṭṭhikau dhammau’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi  khau, āvusau, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sandiṭṭhikau dhammau vuttau bhagavatā nippariyāyainā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sandiṭṭhikanibbānasuttaṃ

47. ‘‘‘sandiṭṭhikaṃ  nibbānaṃ sandiṭṭhikaṃ nibbāna’nti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, sandiṭṭhikaṃ nibbānaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sandiṭṭhikaṃ nibbānaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā pariyāyaina…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, sandiṭṭhikaṃ nibbānaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā nippariyāyainā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. nibbānasuttaṃ

48. ‘‘‘nibbānaṃ  nibbāna’nti, āvusau, vuc‍cati…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. parinibbānasuttaṃ

49. ‘‘‘parinibbānaṃ parinibbāna’nti…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tadaṅganibbānasuttaṃ

50. ‘‘‘tadaṅganibbānaṃ  tadaṅganibbāna’nti, āvusau, vuc‍cati…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. diṭṭhadhammanibbānasuttaṃ

51. ‘‘‘diṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ diṭṭhadhammanibbāna’nti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau diṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi  …pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, diṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā pariyāyaina  …pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, diṭṭhadhammanibbānaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā nippariyāyainā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sāmañ‍ñavaggau pañ‍camau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sambādhau  kāyasakkhī pañ‍ñā,

ubhataubhāgau sandiṭṭhikā dvai|

nibbānaṃ parinibbānaṃ,

tadaṅgadiṭṭhadhammikaina cāti||

paṭhamapaṇṇāsakaṃ samattaṃ|



2. dutiyapaṇṇāsakaṃ


(6) 1. khaimavaggau

1. khaimasuttaṃ

52. ‘‘‘khaimaṃ  khaima’nti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, āvusau, khaimaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, khaimaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā pariyāyaina…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, khaimaṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā nippariyāyainā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. khaimappattasuttaṃ

53. khaimappattau khaimappattauti, āvusau, vuc‍cati…pai॰…| dutiyaṃ|

3. amatasuttaṃ

54. amataṃ amatanti, āvusau, vuc‍cati…pai॰…| tatiyaṃ|

4. amatappattasuttaṃ

55. amatappattau amatappattauti, āvusau, vuc‍cati…pai॰…| catutthaṃ|

5. abhayasuttaṃ

56. abhayaṃ  abhayanti, āvusau, vuc‍cati…pai॰…| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. abhayappattasuttaṃ

57. abhayappattau abhayappattauti, āvusau, vuc‍cati…pai॰…| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. passaddhisuttaṃ

58. passaddhi passaddhīti, āvusau, vuc‍cati…pai॰…| sattamaṃ|

8. anupubbapassaddhisuttaṃ

59. anupubbapassaddhi  anupubbapassaddhīti, āvusau, vuc‍cati…pai॰…| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. niraudhasuttaṃ

60. niraudhau niraudhauti, āvusau, vuc‍cati…pai॰…| navamaṃ|

10. anupubbaniraudhasuttaṃ

61. ‘‘‘anupubbaniraudhau anupubbaniraudhau’ti, āvusau, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā  nu khau, āvusau, anupubbaniraudhau vuttau bhagavatā’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihi…pai॰… paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, anupubbaniraudhau vuttau bhagavatā pariyāyaina…pai॰…|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, āvusau, bhikkhu sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, pañ‍ñāya cassa disvā āsavā parikkhīṇā haunti| aittāvatāpi khau, āvusau, anupubbaniraudhau vuttau bhagavatā nippariyāyainā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. abhabbasuttaṃ

62. ‘‘nava, bhikkhavai, dhammai appahāya abhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai nava? rāgaṃ, dausaṃ, mauhaṃ, kaudhaṃ, upanāhaṃ, makkhaṃ, paḷāsaṃ, issaṃ, macchariyaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, nava dhammai appahāya abhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ|

‘‘nava, bhikkhavai, dhammai pahāya bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātuṃ| katamai nava? rāgaṃ, dausaṃ, mauhaṃ, kaudhaṃ, upanāhaṃ, makkhaṃ, paḷāsaṃ, issaṃ, macchariyaṃ – imai khau, bhikkhavai, nava dhammai pahāya bhabbau arahattaṃ sacchikātu’’nti| aikādasamaṃ|

khaimavaggau paṭhamau|


tassuddānaṃ –

khaimau  ca amatañ‍caiva, abhayaṃ passaddhiyaina ca|

niraudhau anupubbau ca, dhammaṃ pahāya bhabbaina cāti||


(7) 2. satipaṭṭhānavaggau

1. sikkhādubbalyasuttaṃ

63. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, sikkhādubbalyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? pāṇātipātau, adin‍nādānaṃ, kāmaisumicchācārau, musāvādau, surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sikkhādubbalyāni|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ sikkhādubbalyānaṃ pahānāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ sikkhādubbalyānaṃ pahānāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. nīvaraṇasuttaṃ

64. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, nīvaraṇāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ, byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ, thinamiddhanīvaraṇaṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇaṃ, vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ nīvaraṇānaṃ pahānāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰…  dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ nīvaraṇānaṃ pahānāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. kāmaguṇasuttaṃ

65. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, kāmaguṇā| katamai pañ‍ca? cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyā rūpā iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā, sautaviñ‍ñaiyyā saddā…pai॰… ghānaviñ‍ñaiyyā gandhā… jivhāviñ‍ñaiyyā rasā… kāyaviñ‍ñaiyyā phauṭṭhabbā  iṭṭhā kantā manāpā piyarūpā kāmūpasaṃhitā rajanīyā| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ kāmaguṇānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. upādānakkhandhasuttaṃ

66. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau – imai  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. aurambhāgiyasuttaṃ

67. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, vicikicchā, sīlabbataparāmāsau, kāmacchandau, byāpādau – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni|

‘‘imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. gatisuttaṃ

68. ‘‘pañ‍cimā, bhikkhavai, gatiyau| katamā pañ‍ca? nirayau, tiracchānayauni, paittivisayau, manussā, daivā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca gatiyau|

‘‘imāsaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ gatīnaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. macchariyasuttaṃ

69. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, macchariyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? āvāsamacchariyaṃ, kulamacchariyaṃ, lābhamacchariyaṃ, vaṇṇamacchariyaṃ, dhammamacchariyaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca macchariyāni|

‘‘imaisaṃ  khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ macchariyānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. uddhambhāgiyasuttaṃ

70. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? rūparāgau, arūparāgau, mānau, uddhac‍caṃ, avij‍jā – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cuddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. caitaukhilasuttaṃ

71. a॰ ni॰ 5.205; dī॰ ni॰ 3.319; ma॰ ni॰ 1.185 ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, caitaukhilā caitaukhīlā (ka॰)| katamai pañ‍ca? idha bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu satthari kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati na sampasīdati, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, ayaṃ paṭhamau caitaukhilau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhammai kaṅkhati…pai॰… saṅghai kaṅkhati… sikkhāya kaṅkhati… sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sabrahmacārīsu kupitau hauti anattamanau āhatacittau khilajātau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, ayaṃ pañ‍camau caitaukhilau|

‘‘imaisaṃ, khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ caitaukhilānaṃ pahānāya…pai॰… imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. caitasauvinibandhasuttaṃ

72. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, caitasauvinibandhā caitauvinibaddhā (sāratthadīpanīṭīkā) a॰ ni॰ 5.206; dī॰ ni॰ 3.320| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmaisu avītarāgau hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau  avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmaisu avītarāgau hauti avigatacchandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, ayaṃ paṭhamau caitasauvinibandhau|

‘‘puna caparaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai avītarāgau hauti…pai॰… rūpai avītarāgau hauti… yāvadatthaṃ udarāvadaihakaṃ bhuñ‍jitvā saiyyasukhaṃ passasukhaṃ middhasukhaṃ anuyuttau viharati  … añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau  vā’ti| yau sau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu añ‍ñataraṃ daivanikāyaṃ paṇidhāya brahmacariyaṃ carati – ‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā’ti, tassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya| yassa cittaṃ na namati ātappāya anuyaugāya sātac‍cāya padhānāya, ayaṃ pañ‍camau caitasauvinibandhau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ caitasauvinibandhānaṃ pahānāya cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ; vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu dhammānupassī viharati ātāpī sampajānau satimā vinaiyya laukai abhijjhādaumanassaṃ| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ caitasauvinibandhānaṃ pahānāya imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

satipaṭṭhānavaggau dutiyau|


tassuddānaṃ –

sikkhā  nīvaraṇākāmā, khandhā ca aurambhāgiyā gati|

macchairaṃ uddhambhāgiyā aṭṭhamaṃ, caitaukhilā vinibandhāti||


(8) 3. sammappadhānavaggau

1. sikkhasuttaṃ

73. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, sikkhādubbalyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? pāṇātipātau  …pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sikkhādubbalyāni|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ sikkhādubbalyānaṃ pahānāya cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ sikkhādubbalyānaṃ pahānāya imai cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

74-81. (yathā satipaṭṭhānavaggai tathā sammappadhānavasaina vitthāraitabbā|)

10. caitasauvinibandhasuttaṃ

82. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, caitasauvinibandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmaisu avītarāgau hauti…pai॰… imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ caitasauvinibandhānaṃ pahānāya cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anuppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ anuppādāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati  vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati; uppan‍nānaṃ pāpakānaṃ akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānāya… anuppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādāya… uppan‍nānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ṭhitiyā asammausāya bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya bhāvanāya pāripūriyā chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati vīriyaṃ ārabhati cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ caitasauvinibandhānaṃ pahānāya imai cattārau sammappadhānā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

sammappadhānavaggau tatiyau|



(9) 4. iddhipādavaggau

1. sikkhasuttaṃ

83. ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, sikkhādubbalyāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? pāṇātipātau…pai॰… surāmairayamaj‍japamādaṭṭhānaṃ – imāni khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca sikkhādubbalyāni|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ sikkhādubbalyānaṃ pahānāya cattārau iddhipādā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi… cittasamādhi… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ sikkhādubbalyānaṃ pahānāya imai cattārau iddhipādā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

84-91. (yathā satipaṭṭhānavaggai tathā iddhipādavasaina vitthāraitabbā|)

10. caitasauvinibandhasuttaṃ

92. ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, caitasauvinibandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu kāmaisu avītarāgau hauti…pai॰… imai khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca caitasauvinibandhā|

‘‘imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ caitasauvinibandhānaṃ pahānāya imai cattārau iddhipādā bhāvaitabbā| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu chandasamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ bhāvaiti, vīriyasamādhi… cittasamādhi… vīmaṃsāsamādhipadhānasaṅkhārasaman‍nāgataṃ iddhipādaṃ  bhāvaiti| imaisaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, pañ‍can‍naṃ caitasauvinibandhānaṃ pahānāya imai cattārau iddhipādā bhāvaitabbā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

iddhipādavaggau catutthau|


yathaiva  satipaṭṭhānā, padhānā caturaupi ca|

cattārau iddhipādā ca, tathaiva sampayaujayaiti||


(10) 5. rāgapaiyyālaṃ

93. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya nava dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai nava? asubhasañ‍ñā, maraṇasañ‍ñā, āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñā, sabbalaukai anabhiratasañ‍ñā, anic‍casañ‍ñā, anic‍cai dukkhasañ‍ñā, dukkhai anattasañ‍ñā, pahānasañ‍ñā, virāgasañ‍ñā – rāgassa, bhikkhavai abhiñ‍ñāya imai nava dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

94. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya nava dhammā bhāvaitabbā| katamai  nava? paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, catutthaṃ jhānaṃ, ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ, naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ, sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhau – rāgassa, bhikkhavai, abhiñ‍ñāya imai nava dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

95-112. ‘‘rāgassa, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñāya…pai॰… parikkhayāya…pai॰… pahānāya…pai॰… khayāya…pai॰… vayāya…pai॰… virāgāya…pai॰… niraudhāya…pai॰… cāgāya…pai॰… paṭinissaggāya…pai॰… imai nava dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’|

113-432. ‘‘dausassa…pai॰… mauhassa… kaudhassa… upanāhassa… makkhassa… paḷāsassa… issāya… macchariyassa… māyāya… sāṭhaiyyassa… thambhassa… sārambhassa… mānassa… atimānassa… madassa… pamādassa abhiñ‍ñāya…pai॰… pariñ‍ñāya… parikkhayāya… pahānāya… khayāya… vayāya… virāgāya… niraudhāya … cāgāya… paṭinissaggāya…pai॰… imai nava dhammā bhāvaitabbā’’ti|

rāgapaiyyālaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|




navakanipātapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
